  - `src/client/ui/help.ts` (a help line), `docs/features.md`.

### Working at the boss desk

At the boss's PC up in the loft you can work, not only play: sitting in the boss's chair, **E** offers a Claude worker, a shell or Minesweeper. The boss desk (`BOSS_DESK`, id `boss`, flag `boss`) is a real place for a worker, but only hired at by hand: it isn't in `SEATS`, so `nextFreeSeat`, the queue, bean bag counts and the castle's seats never see it, and workers hiring workers (`/office/workers`) can't pick it. Nobody is drawn sitting there (the chair is yours): the desk has no `DeskView`, so `syncWorkers` skips its worker, and its terminal plays on the boss's monitor, with Minesweeper back on it once it's gone home. Guests only play (or watch a terminal that's up there). Office map only: the server refuses it on other maps.

- `src/client/ui/bossdesk.ts`: the chooser, the hint's words, and the monitor showing the boss desk's terminal; `src/client/features/bossdesk/index.ts`: makes it on the boss's monitor and updates it. `tests/bossdesk.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/layout.ts`: `DeskDef.boss`/`DeskDef.y`, `BOSS_DESK` (after `LOFT`), and in `DESK_BY_ID`.
  - `src/shared/maps/index.ts`: `BOSS_DESK` in the office plan's `byId`.
  - `src/server/ws/handlers/workers.ts`: `worker.spawn` refuses the boss desk off the office map.
  - `src/server/office-workers.ts`: `readHireRequest` refuses `desk: 'boss'`.
  - `src/server/dog.ts`: the dog doesn't bark at or nap by the boss desk (it doesn't do stairs).
  - `src/client/main.ts`: `installBossDesk(...)`; `src/client/core/parts.ts`: `bossDesk`; `src/client/core/maps.ts`: stopped with the arcade.
  - `src/client/features/seating/index.ts`: the boss's chair's E opens it (`bossDesk().open()`), the seat hint (`useLabel`, `aside`).
  - `src/client/features/workers/actions.ts`: `standAt` goes up to the loft (`desk.y`), `openShell`/`killWorker` returned for it; `src/client/features/workers/views.ts`: the confetti burst too.
  - `tests/maps.test.ts`: the built-in maps have every office seat but the boss desk.
  - `docs/features.md`: the boss desk line.

### Radio stations on the jukebox

The jukebox is also a DAB-style radio: a list of stations (Radio BOB!, BAYERN 3, Antenne Bayern, Bayern 1, FluxFM, radioeins, Deutschlandfunk, DLF Nova, 1LIVE, SWR3, egoFM, Jazz Radio, I Love Chillhop, BR-KLASSIK) to click, for everyone on the floor, and the toast names the station. The box for your own stream stays; a `.pls`/`.m3u` link plays the first stream it lists.

An `http://` stream can't play on the https office (mixed content), so it goes through `/api/radio`, which passes the audio on as it comes. That is no open proxy: it streams only a built-in station (`?station=`) or the stream on that floor's jukebox right now (`?floor=&u=`), never an address on this machine or its network (checked after DNS, on every connection and redirect), at most 24 at once, and drops the station when the listener goes. https streams play straight from the station, with the office as fallback. When the browser won't start the radio before a click, a toast says to click.

- `src/shared/radio.ts`: the stations (each checked with curl for HTTP 200 and `audio/mpeg`) and where the page loads a stream from (`radioSources`).
- `src/server/radio.ts`: the proxy, what it may fetch (`radioTarget`), the address check, playlists; `tests/radio.test.ts`.
- `src/server/http/routes/fork.ts`: the `/api/radio` route (`forkRoutes.radio`); `ctx.radio` (a `RadioProxy`) is made in `src/server/fork/office.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/jukebox.ts`: `JukeboxState.station`; `trackTitle` names the station.
  - `src/shared/protocol/toys.ts`: `station` on `jukebox.play`.
  - `src/server/jukebox.ts`: `play({ station })`, the station saved and loaded.
  - `src/server/http/routes/index.ts`: `forkRoutes.radio` in `routes`. `src/server/ws/handlers/jukebox.ts`: `jukebox.play` passes `station` and resolves playlists.
  - `src/server/guests.ts`: guests may fetch `/api/radio` for a station or a floor's stream (`tests/guests.test.ts` lists the route).
  - `src/client/features/jukebox/ui.ts`: the station list. `src/client/styles/base.css`: `.jb-radio`.
  - `src/client/features/jukebox/index.ts`: `radioSources` for what the jukebox plays. `src/client/core/you.ts`: the click-to-hear toast (`onMusicBlocked`).
  - `src/client/features/jukebox/sound.ts`: `fallback` and `onBlocked`; `src/client/sound/index.ts`: `onMusicBlocked`.
  - `docs/features.md`: the jukebox line.

### DJ sets on the roof

Anyone on the roof, guests too, can paste a YouTube, SoundCloud or Mixcloud link at the DJ booth (**E** there opens its window; **H** there is now the air horn). It plays for everyone up there, from the same moment, in place of the synthesized house DJ, until someone sends the house DJ back. The office keeps what's on in `dj.json` in its data folder; each browser plays it in the site's own embedded player, off the page, turned up or down for how far it stands from the booth. A browser that can't play it (embedding turned off, blocked) falls back to the house DJ by itself. When the set runs out, the roof goes quiet rather than back to the house DJ (Florian: *lieber ist es dann mal still*): a toast says so once, the booth's hint and window say the set is over, the lights and the LED wall drift slowly with nothing hitting (`quietFrame` in `features/djset/frame.ts`), until someone puts on the next set or sends the house DJ back (`DjSetPlayer.over()`; `silencesHouse()` only lets the house DJ back in for a page that can't play the set). The Schallwerk's sets still hand back to its house mix.

- `src/shared/djset.ts`: reading a pasted link (only those three sites, by exact host), `tests/djset.test.ts`.
- `src/server/djset.ts`: the booth (`DjBooth`: what's on, saved; its title from the site's oEmbed) and `djMessage` (dj.play/dj.stop: only from the roof, not too often).
- `src/client/djset.ts`: the embedded players and keeping them in step; `src/client/ui/djbooth.ts`: the booth's window.
- `src/client/features/djset/index.ts`: `djSets`/`houseDj()`, the `dj` message and `msg.dj` on arrival, `setUp`, `showDjBooth()`, H at the booth (an `activity` key), what the booth's hint says is on (`playing`).
- Server: `djBooth` on the `Fork` context and `roofExtras` (the roof view carries `dj`) in `src/server/fork/office.ts`; `dj.play`/`dj.stop` in `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts`; the messages in `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/protocol/floors.ts`: `FloorView.dj`.
  - `src/server/office/views.ts`: `roofView` adds `roofExtras`. `src/server/ws/handlers/index.ts`: `dj: noView` in `views` (a floor has none).
  - `src/server/guests.ts`: `dj.play`, `dj.stop` in `GUEST`.
  - `src/client/sound/index.ts`: `djSetVolume()` (`sound/screens.ts`).
  - `src/client/core/travel.ts`: `setPlace` calls `parts.djset.setUp(up)` in place of `sound.setDj`.

### The roof moves to the set that's on

The LED wall behind the DJ, the lights and the DJ go by the DJ set someone put on, not only by the house DJ. The office hears each set: `yt-dlp` fetches its audio into a throwaway folder, `ffmpeg` (or, without it, macOS' `afconvert`, for AAC and MP3) makes it mono samples, and the office finds its beats, how hard each hits, and where its breakdowns, builds and drops are; the audio is deleted at once, and only that (a few dozen kB per hour) is kept, in `dj-beats/` in its data folder (the last 60 sets). Every browser on the roof fetches it and turns it into the same `DjFrame` the house DJ gives, at the point in the set its player is at. Until it's heard (or if it can't be: a live stream, a SoundCloud playlist, no `yt-dlp`), a steady groove at 124 BPM; anyone can tap the tempo in at the booth (🥁 **Tap**, or **T** in its window), which then goes before what was heard, for everyone, until **↺ The set's own beat**. The LED wall is a shader of round LEDs: eight programs (tunnel, kaleidoscope, spectrum, synthwave sun, warp, checkerboard, rings, plasma) taking turns by the part and the bars, FLOGGE OFFICE as a drop lands, GET READY and a countdown through a build, the set's title in a breakdown, its tempo now and then. The DJ spins and jumps as a drop lands, pumps their fists, bangs their head, points up and claps overhead through a build, and sways with their hands up in a breakdown.

- Needs `yt-dlp` on the Mac (`brew install yt-dlp`; `ffmpeg` is optional). Looked for in Homebrew's folders too (a launch agent's PATH has none), or set `AGENT_OFFICE_YTDLP` / `AGENT_OFFICE_FFMPEG`. Installed after the office started: the next set finds it.
- `src/shared/djbeats.ts`: what's heard (`DjBeats`), how hearing it goes (`DjBeatsStatus`, on `DjSetState.beats`), a tapped tempo (`DjTap`, `DjSetState.tap`).
- `src/server/djbeats/analyse.ts` (the listening: three bands, onsets, tempo by autocorrelation, an Ellis-style beat path, bars, parts), `fetch.ts` (yt-dlp, ffmpeg/afconvert), `index.ts` (`DjBeatsJobs`: one set at a time, kept by link). `DjBooth` in `src/server/djset.ts` asks for the set that's on and carries the status and the tap; `dj.tap` in `djMessage`; `GET /api/dj/beats` in `src/server/http/routes/fork.ts` (guests and party guests may fetch it); `onBeats` set in `startFork`.
- `src/client/features/djset/frame.ts` (`SetBeats`, `gridFrame`), `frame()` and the tap in `features/djset/index.ts` (the set's player's own position corrects the office clock's, every 2 s); the tempo row in `src/client/ui/djbooth.ts`.
- `src/client/features/rooftop/ledwall.ts` (the LED wall), `dancer.ts` (the DJ, moved out of `world.ts`).
- `tests/djbeats.test.ts`: a made-up track's tempo, beats, breakdown, build and drop; the booth hearing and letting go; tapping; the frame.
- **Disco (past 100%):** the party volume goes to 200% (🪩 **Disco!** jumps there, ↩︎ back). Past 100% the house DJ is driven into a limiter (louder, never clipping), lifted for anyone whose own music volume is lower (to what their speakers take; muted stays muted), and fades far less with distance, so the whole terrace has it; sets in embedded players get the same lift and fall-off up to the player's 100%. `src/client/sound/party.ts` (the curves), a limiter and a lift gain in `features/bar/sound.ts` (hook lines), `djSetVolume` in `sound/screens.ts`, `setMusicVolume` in `sound/index.ts` passes your music volume on.
- **Skipping in the set:** the booth's window (and the Schallwerk's DJ desk) has a slider over the set's length with where everyone is (19:34 / 1:11:45), and ⏪ 30 s, ⏩ 30 s, 2 min, 5 min, for everyone at once. `dj.seek` (guests may, like `dj.play`; from the roof, at most every 400 ms each) moves when the set started (`LinkPlayer.seek` in `server/embeds.ts`, never before the link's own start, saved); every page's player jumps in place (`EmbedPlayer.apply` in `client/embeds.ts` keeps the deck when only `startedAt` changed), and the lights and the LED wall's video follow by themselves. A SoundCloud set (a playlist) can't be skipped (`djSetSeekable`). The controls are `skipControls` in `src/client/ui/djcontrols.ts`, shared by both desks; the party volume's controls are `partyVolumeControls` there too. `tests/djset.test.ts`.
- **Party volume:** the booth's window has a slider (and 🔇 Silence) setting how loud the house DJ and every set are for everyone on the roof, on top of each person's own volume. The team sets it (`dj.volume` is in `TEAM_ONLY`), from anywhere; guests and party guests see it. `DjBooth.setVolume` keeps it in `dj-volume.json`; it rides on `DjSetState.volume`/`volumeBy`. Client: `setPartyVolume` in `sound/index.ts` (fork section) scales `djSetVolume()` and, through a gain in `features/bar/sound.ts` (hook lines), the house DJ.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/client/features/rooftop/world.ts`: the DJ comes from `dancer.ts`, the LED wall from `ledwall.ts` (wider and taller, 9.6 × 4.6 m; the old canvas wall and its words are gone).
  - `src/client/features/rooftop/index.ts`: `setFrame` in its deps (the set's frame before the house DJ's); `src/client/main.ts` passes `parts.djset.frame()`.
  - `src/server/guests.ts`: `dj.tap` in `GUEST`, `/api/dj/beats` in `guestMayFetch`. `src/server/http/routes/index.ts`: `forkRoutes.djBeats`.
  - `src/client/features/bar/index.ts`: E at the booth opens `showDjBooth()`, the booth's hint (E for a set, H for the horn), `blowHorn` returned.
  - `src/client/main.ts`: `installDjSets(...)`, `djset` for the bar; `src/client/core/parts.ts`: `djset`.
  - `src/client/ui/help.ts`, `docs/features.md`, `docs/controls.md`, `docs/how-it-works.md`: words.

### The set's video on the LED wall

While a YouTube set plays, the LED wall behind the DJ shows the set's own video now and then, LED by LED, as a real LED wall would. A browser can't draw YouTube's player into WebGL (another site's frame), so the office fetches a small copy of the video, without sound, once the set's beats are heard (never alongside, so the lights get theirs first; one at a time, stopped when the set changes): `yt-dlp` asks for the smallest H.264 MP4 there is up to 360 lines (`bv*[height<=144][vcodec^=avc1][ext=mp4][protocol=https]`, then 240, then 360), the one kind Safari and Chrome both play with no `ffmpeg` to convert it; at 144p an hour is about 50 MB (at most 400 MB, 20 minutes to fetch). The last three are kept in `dj-video/` in the data folder (by link, mode 0600; a set put on again is ready at once). Every browser on the roof plays it in a hidden, muted `<video>` from `GET /api/dj/video` (in pieces, HTTP Range), kept to where the set you hear is (`expectedAt` plus how far the set's player here is off): more than 0.6 s off it jumps there, else it runs up to 3% faster or slower till it's caught up; it pauses while the set's player does (not started yet, blocked), shows nothing until it has a picture in step, and is let go of off the roof, when the set changes, and for the house DJ. On the wall (`video`, a ninth program) it's one sample per LED, letterboxed to the wall, a little more colourful and contrasty, pulsing with the kick, with the words still over it. When, by the frame alone so every screen agrees: in a groove or a breakdown 16 or 32 bars of every 64, in a drop the first two bars of each sixteen (never while FLOGGE OFFICE flashes as it lands), never through a build; with reduce motion the video stays on in place of the plasma. SoundCloud and Mixcloud sets, a live stream, or a video that can't be fetched or played: the wall goes on as before. The booth's window says how it's going ("📺 fetching the set's video for the LED wall…", "… comes on the LED wall now and then", or why there's none).

- `src/server/djvideo/fetch.ts` (yt-dlp, `VIDEO_FORMAT`), `index.ts` (`DjVideoJobs`: one at a time, kept by link, the last three), `serve.ts` (`parseRange`, `serveVideo`: 200, 206, 416, HEAD). `DjBooth` in `src/server/djset.ts` starts it once the beats are done (`wantVideo`), carries `DjSetState.video` (`DjVideoStatus` in `src/shared/djset.ts`: pending, ready with its `key`, failed with why) and tells the roof through `onBeats`; `videoFile()` for the route. `run` in `djbeats/fetch.ts` is exported for it.
- `GET /api/dj/video` in `src/server/http/routes/fork.ts`: only ever the set that's on (no path in the request; `?v=<key>` only keeps browsers' caches apart), 404 without one.
- `src/client/features/djset/video.ts` (`SetVideo`: the `<video>`, its `VideoTexture`, keeping it in step); `frame()` in `features/djset/index.ts` hands it to the wall on the frame (`video`), `setUp` lets go of it off the roof.
- `src/client/features/rooftop/videoturn.ts` (`videoTurn`: when it's the video's turn), the `video` program in `rooftop/ledwall.ts` (its texture bound only while it's on the wall, so it's only uploaded then; `window.__ledWall.force('video')` / `.now()` from the console, to look at one program).
- The status line in `src/client/ui/djbooth.ts`. `tests/djvideo.test.ts`: fetching after the beats and letting go, no video for SoundCloud and Mixcloud, failing once, keeping three, the format, Range, guests, the turns.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/server/http/routes/index.ts`: `forkRoutes.djVideo`. `src/server/guests.ts`: `/api/dj/video` in `guestMayFetch` (party guests too).
  - `docs/features.md`: words.

### The casino

Diagonally across the street from the office (x -46..-20, z 36..56, where two neighbours stood) there's a casino: a burgundy block with a neon CASINO sign ringed with chaser bulbs, an awning over a red carpet, and glass doors that slide apart. **E** at the doors goes in; **E** at the doors inside comes back out onto the street of the floor you came from. Inside is a place of its own like the roof (`CASINO = '@casino'` is your `floor` while you're in there), so people from every floor meet at its tables, guests too. Play chips only: nothing to buy, nothing to cash out. Everyone starts with 1,000; once a day (office clock, local midnight) anyone below 1,000 is topped back up to it. Four games, all decided on the server (`crypto.randomInt`): eight slot machines along the west wall (RTP 94.99 %, checked by exact enumeration); a shared European roulette wheel whose rounds run by themselves while anyone is seated (bets 20 s, spin 6 s, payout 5 s; every inside and outside bet; house edge exactly 1/37); two blackjack tables (six-deck shoe, dealer stands on soft 17, blackjack pays 3:2, double, split once, the hole card never leaves the server early); and no-limit Texas Hold'em for 2–6 (buy-in 100–1000 from your wallet, blinds 5/10, side pots, a full 7-card evaluator, 30 s turns, your stack goes back to your wallet when you get up or leave; an optional "🤖 House" bot plays with the house's own chips). Party guests may play too (`src/server/party.ts` passes the casino's messages).

- Own files:
  - `src/shared/casino.ts`: `CASINO`, the building's box, the room, the door, where you land coming in and going out, `CASINO_TABLES` (every table and machine: id, kind, position, seats), the chip rules (`START_CHIPS`, `MIN_BET`/`MAX_BET`, `validBet`), and the generic table protocol (`CasinoClientMsg`, `CasinoServerMsg`).
  - `src/shared/casino-slots.ts`: symbols, weighted reel strips, paytable (RTP 94.99%, exact), `SlotsView`/`SlotsResult`.
  - `src/server/casino/`: `index.ts` (`Casino`: who's inside, seating, rate limits, `crypto.randomInt`, the 250 ms tick, sending views), `wallets.ts` (`casino.json` in the data folder, 0600, written atomically, keyed by `account:<id>` or `name:<name>`), `game.ts` (the `CasinoGame` interface), `slots.ts` (`SlotMachine`), `soon.ts` (`ComingSoon`).
  - `src/client/casino.ts` (`CasinoPlace`: in and out, the room, the chip counter, the table windows, hints, lighting inside), `src/client/world/casino/` (`exterior.ts`, also the block the roof's city shows; `interior.ts`; `parts.ts`), `src/client/ui/casino/` (`registry.ts`, `slots.ts`, `soon.ts`, `reels.ts`, `casino.css`).
  - `tests/casino-wallet.test.ts`, `tests/casino-slots.test.ts`, `tests/casino-tables.test.ts`.
- The protocol: you go in and out with `floor.go` (`CASINO`; back with your floor's id and `at` in front of the doors). At a table it's always the same three messages, whatever the game: `casino.sit {table}`, `casino.stand`, `casino.act {table, action, data}`; the office answers with `casino.wallet {chips, nextTopUpAt?}`, `casino.table {table, state}` (the game's own view, per viewer: hide hole cards there) and `casino.result {table, text, delta?, data?}`. All three are `GUEST`.
- **Phase 2 (roulette, blackjack, poker):** no new messages, no new hooks. Server: a class implementing `CasinoGame` (`src/server/casino/game.ts`) with the table's id, made for its tables in `GAMES` (`src/server/casino/index.ts`: `roulette: (t) => new Roulette(t),` in place of the `ComingSoon`). Chips only move through `ctx.stake` (checks whole chips, limits, the balance) and `ctx.pay`; randomness through `ctx.random`; timed things (a betting window, the dealer's turn) in `tick`; `ctx.changed()` sends the view to everyone inside. Client: a window `(ctx: CasinoUiContext) => CasinoUi` registered with `registerCasinoUi(kind, open)` (`src/client/ui/casino/registry.ts`) in a file imported from `client/casino.ts`, like `ui/casino/slots.ts`; the 3D tables are in `world/casino/interior.ts` (`setTable` gets every table's state, for animating a wheel or dealt cards).
- **Poker (phase 2):** no-limit Texas Hold'em, a cash game among whoever sits at the `poker` table (2–6). You sit down with nothing and buy in from your wallet (`buyin`, 100–1000, default 200; topping up between hands to at most 1000); getting up, closing the window or leaving puts your stack back in your wallet (mid-hand: you're folded, what you'd already bet stays in the pot, an uncalled bet of yours comes back). Blinds 5/10, the button moves round (heads-up the button posts the small blind and acts first before the flop), a hand starts by itself 5 s after the last once two have chips. Actions `fold`, `check`, `call`, `raise` (data: the total to go to this round), `allin`, plus `sitout`/`back` and `bot`. Minimum bet the big blind, minimum raise the last full raise; an all-in short of a full raise doesn't reopen the betting for those who already acted. 30 s per turn (then a check, or a fold facing a bet; two in a row sit you out). Uncalled bets go back, side pots per all-in level, the odd chip of a split to the first winner left of the button, an all-in with nobody left to bet turns the cards up and runs the board out. Views show your own hole cards and, at a showdown, those of everyone still in; never the deck. Optional house bot ("🤖 House", `bot` toggles it) so one person can play: its 1000-chip stack is the house's (made and lost from nothing, never a wallet), it gives its chair to a sixth person and leaves with the last one.
  - `src/shared/casino-poker.ts` (rules' numbers, cards, the 7-card evaluator, `splitPots`/`shareOut`, `PokerView`), `src/server/casino/poker.ts` (`PokerTable`), `src/client/ui/casino/poker.ts` + `poker.css` (the window: oval table, action bar with slider and ½ pot / pot / all-in, buy-in; F/C/R keys), `src/client/world/casino/poker-felt.ts` (the felt in the room shows the board and the pot), `tests/casino-poker.test.ts`.
- The places across the street (the casino, the gym, the padel hall, the soccer hall) share their plumbing:
  - Server, `src/server/fork/office.ts`: each place's state on the `Fork` context (made in `createFork`, stopped in `stopFork`), `casinoPlayer`/`gymPlayer`, `PLACES`/`isPlace`, `placeView`, `goToPlace` (`floor.go` to a place, just inside its door), `backInPlace` (back in after a reload) and `enteredPlace`. Their messages go to `forkHandlers`, leaving and closing to `forkHooks` (`src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts`).
  - Client, `src/client/features/places/index.ts`: makes the four places (`CasinoPlace`, `GymPlace`, `HallPlace`, `SoccerPlace`) and `PadelPlay`, declares and defines their kinds (`casino`, `casinotable`, `gym`, `gymstation`, `hall`, `cafe`, `padel`, `soccer`, `soccerpitch`, each with its reach and hint), routes every message to them, `arrived()` after welcome and floor.enter, their `update` and `mood` ticks, and hands the rest of the client `active`, `usable`, `pickables`, `setPlace`, `refresh`, `title`, `hides`, `seesYou`.
  - Their buildings on the street: `casinoOut`, `gymOut`, `hallOut`, `soccerOut` in `src/client/world/office/fork.ts` (door in `doors`, `setStreet` in `setLevel`, `update`), listed in `src/client/world/office/build.ts`.
  - Shared hook lines: `src/shared/protocol.ts` (`ForkClientMsg`/`ForkServerMsg`, from `protocol/fork.ts`, in `ClientMsg`/`ServerMsg`); `src/server/server.ts` (`createFork`, `startFork`, `stopFork`); `src/server/office/context.ts` (`Fork` in `Ctx`, `Navigation.goSomewhere`); `src/server/office/navigation.ts` (`goSomewhere`, through `leave`); `src/server/ws/handlers/floors.ts` (`goToPlace` first in `floor.go`); `src/server/ws/connection.ts` (`isPlace`, `backInPlace`, `placeView`, `enteredPlace`); `src/server/ws/handlers/index.ts` (`...forkHandlers`, `forkHooks` in `features`); `src/client/main.ts` (`installPlaces(...)`, `__office.padel`/`soccer`); `src/client/core/parts.ts` (`places`); `src/client/core/travel.ts` (`placeTrip` in and out with the lights dipping, `setPlace`, the garage ride from inside); `src/client/core/maps.ts` (`refresh` in `applyMap`); `src/client/core/arrival.ts` (the project corner's `title()`); `src/client/input/pointer.ts` (`usable`, `pickables`); `src/client/core/loop.ts` (`seesYou`); `src/client/world/types.ts` (the `Interactable` fields).
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/server/guests.ts`: `casino.sit`, `casino.stand`, `casino.act` in `GUEST`.
  - `src/client/world/types.ts`: `Interactable.casinoTable`.
  - `src/client/world/outside.ts`: the two neighbours across the street to the west are gone, `neighbourBoxes()` has the casino (golf balls and the scenic loop's trees keep clear of it), and one far-side street lamp moved from x -34 to -37, out of the doorway.
  - `src/client/world/city.ts`: the roof's city leaves the casino's lot free and draws `cityCasino()` there.
  - `src/client/sound/casino.ts` (fork file) and `casino(kind)` in `src/client/sound/index.ts`. `src/client/ui/whereabouts.ts` ("🎰 in the casino"), `src/client/ui/people.ts` ("🎰 Casino").
  - `docs/features.md`: the casino line.

### The gym

Across the street from the office to the east (x 6..34, z 36..56, where two neighbours stood — the casino took the two to the west), built exactly like the casino: a steel-and-glass fitness club with a lime neon FITNESS sign and glass doors that slide apart. **E** at the doors goes in; **E** at the doors inside comes back out onto the street of the floor you came from. Inside is a place of its own like the roof and the casino (`GYM = '@gym'` is your `floor` while you're in there), so people from every floor meet at its machines, guests too. Nothing costs anything: you work out for fitness points (XP → levels → a leaderboard) and the wellness area gives back the energy (stamina) your workouts spend. Party guests may work out too (`src/server/party.ts` passes the gym's messages).

Four kinds of station, all decided on the server, all speaking the one generic sit/act/stand protocol (mirrors the casino's, so a new machine needs no new message types): a **cardio deck** (treadmills, bikes, a rower, a cross-trainer) whose sessions run on the office's clock, piling up distance and calories and spending energy the harder you push; a **strength floor** (bench, squat, deadlift, leg press, lat pulldown, shoulder press, cable, dumbbells, heavy bag) where the office grinds a chosen set out rep by rep (heavier over your level → the last reps risk breaking form); a **wellness spa** (sauna, steam room, jacuzzi, cold plunge, massage tables, stretch studio) that tops energy back up over time and earns recovery XP; and a **juice bar** (the casino's cashier, but a place to sit) showing your fitness and the building's leaderboard, and pouring a smoothie for a quick top-up. Everyone's fitness is kept in `gym.json` in the data folder.

- **Where it stands:** on the street the gym is `GYM_SHIFT` (52 m) east of its interior's coordinates, `GYM_STREET_BOX` x 58..86, right next to the padel hall (`HALL_BOX` x 22..54), which took the lot the gym was first planned on. Only the building outside uses the street box, door and spot (`GYM_STREET_*`); the interior is a scene of its own and keeps `GYM_BOX`/`GYM_ROOM`. Two far-side street lamps (x 62, 82) light its front. `tests/gym-lot.test.ts` keeps it clear of the hall and the casino.
- Own files:
  - `src/shared/gym.ts`: `GYM`, the building's box, the room, the door, where you land coming in and going out, `GYM_STATIONS` (every machine: id, kind, machine, position, seats), `JUICE_BAR`, the fitness maths (`xpForLevel`/`levelFor`/`rankFor`, `STAMINA_MAX`/`STAMINA_REGEN`, `FitnessProfile`, `LeaderRow`, the smoothies, `JuiceBarView`), and the generic protocol (`GymClientMsg`, `GymServerMsg`, `isGymMsg`).
  - `src/shared/gym-strength.ts`, `gym-cardio.ts`, `gym-wellness.ts`: the exercise/machine/spot catalogs and the pure scoring maths (a set rep by rep, a cardio step, recovery over time) and the views, shared by the server and the tests.
  - `src/server/gym/`: `index.ts` (`Gym`: who's inside, seating, rate limits, the 250 ms tick, sending views — mirrors `server/casino/index.ts`, reusing its `Allowance`), `fitness.ts` (`Fitness`: `gym.json`, XP/levels, energy that regenerates over time, personal bests, the daily streak, the leaderboard; written throttled), `game.ts` (the `GymGame` interface and `GymContext`), `cardio.ts`, `strength.ts`, `wellness.ts`, `juicebar.ts`.
  - `src/client/gym.ts` (`GymPlace`: in and out, the room, the fitness HUD, the station windows, hints, lighting — mirrors `client/casino.ts`), `src/client/world/gym/` (`exterior.ts`, also the block the roof's city shows, and `cityGym`/`onGymLot`; `interior.ts`; `parts.ts`, reusing the casino's generic parts), `src/client/ui/gym/` (`registry.ts`, `cardio.ts`, `strength.ts`, `wellness.ts`, `juicebar.ts`, `gym.css`).
  - `tests/gym-fitness.test.ts`, `tests/gym-workout.test.ts`, `tests/gym-stations.test.ts`.
- The protocol: you go in and out with `floor.go` (`GYM`; back with your floor's id and `at` in front of the doors). At a station it's always the same three messages, whatever the machine: `gym.sit {station}`, `gym.stand`, `gym.act {station, action, data}`; the office answers with `gym.profile {profile}`, `gym.station {station, state}` (the game's own view, per viewer) and `gym.result {station, text, xp?, data?}`. All three client messages are `GUEST` (and pass the party gate).
- A game (`src/server/gym/game.ts`): fitness points and energy only move through the context (`ctx.award`, `ctx.addStamina`, `ctx.countWorkout`, `ctx.setPr`); randomness through `ctx.random`; timed things (a running session, a set finishing, water warming) in `tick`; `ctx.changed()` sends the view to everyone inside; `ctx.leaderboard()` builds the juice bar's board. A client window `(ctx) => GymUi` is registered with `registerGymUi(kind, open)` (`src/client/ui/gym/registry.ts`) in a file imported from `client/gym.ts`; the 3D stations are in `world/gym/interior.ts` (`setStation` gets every station's state, for spinning belts, loading plates and puffing steam).
- Server and client plumbing: as the casino's (`src/server/fork/office.ts`, `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts`, `src/client/features/places/index.ts`, `gymOut` in `src/client/world/office/fork.ts`; see the casino above).
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/server/guests.ts`: `gym.sit`, `gym.stand`, `gym.act` in `GUEST`.
  - `src/server/party.ts`: `gym.profile`, `gym.station`, `gym.result` in `PARTY_SEES`.
  - `src/client/world/types.ts`: `Interactable.gymStation`.
  - `src/client/world/outside.ts`: the two neighbours across the street to the east are gone, `neighbourBoxes()` has the gym, and two far-side street lamps (x 62, 82) light its front.
  - `src/client/world/city.ts`: the roof's city leaves the gym's lot free and draws `cityGym()` there.
  - `src/client/sound/gym.ts` (fork file) and `gym(kind)` in `src/client/sound/index.ts`. `src/client/ui/whereabouts.ts` ("🏋️ in the gym"), `src/client/ui/people.ts` ("🏋️ Gym").
  - `src/client/lab/props.ts`: `gym_interior` (cutaway, no ceiling), `gym_exterior` and `gym_city` for eyeballing the models.
  - `docs/features.md`: the gym line.
- **Equipment** (the cardio and strength machines, and the people on them): every machine is modelled in detail and moves for everyone in the gym, with whoever's on it posed and animated doing the exercise; nothing new on the wire.
  - `src/client/world/gym/machines/`: one builder per machine, each in its own frame (the user at `spot`, facing +z): `treadmill.ts` (slatted belt that scrolls at the session's speed, console with a live screen, safety key, handrails, LED strips), `bike.ts` (spin bike: flywheel, cranks and pedals, saddle, bars, screen), `rower.ts` (sliding seat, fan cage, handle on its chain, monitor), `elliptical.ts` (pedals on their ellipses, swinging poles, flywheel), `bench.ts`, `squat.ts` (power cage, J-hooks, safeties, plate pegs), `deadlift.ts` (platform, bumper-loaded bar, chalk bowl), `legpress.ts` (45° sled, plate horns), `latpull.ts`, `shoulder.ts`, `cable.ts` (crossover, two stacks), `dumbbells.ts` (two-tier rack, the pair in use leaves it), `bag.ts` (heavy bag on a damped pendulum, speed bag, gloves). `kit.ts`: the shared parts (plates, `loadedBar`, `weightStack` with its pin, pulleys, cables, `screen`, `led`), the `Machine` shape (`statics` merged per machine with `mergeByColor`, `live`, `spot`/`off`/`size`/`cam`, `update`, `pose`, optional `gear` and `shift`), and the `Poser` that puts a person on a machine (hips and lean, then a hand or foot at a time onto a point of it: no elbows or knees, so a limb shortens along its length to reach).
  - `src/client/world/gym/equipment.ts`: builds the machines at their stations (colliders, interactables), turns each station's view into motion every frame (the belt's eased speed and cadence, where a set's reps are), finds who's on a station (you, or the person in the gym by that name standing at it), poses them via `Person.setWorkout`, plays the machines' sounds, and frames your camera on one (`frame`, first person on the cardio deck).
  - `src/shared/gym-motion.ts`: the pure maths, shared with the server — the rep tempos (`REP_SECONDS`), `setDurationMs` (the server times a set by it), `setMotion` (lead-in, reps, racking; a broken rep stalls halfway, or is stuck at the bottom on the bench/squat/leg press), `stackPlates`, `barPlates`, `nearestSize`, `cardioHz`, `rowStroke`, `ease`. `tests/gym-motion.test.ts`.
  - The cardio and strength machines left `world/gym/interior.ts` (it calls `buildEquipment` and hands it cardio/strength station states). The cable crossover and dumbbell rack stand `shift`ed 1.7 m / 1.1 m west of their station points, clear of the spa's glass at x 22; `GYM_STATIONS` is unchanged.
  - Timing: `server/gym/strength.ts` holds a set for `setDurationMs` (the lifter's natural tempo, not 340 ms a rep) and sends `since` in `StrengthView`, so onlookers walking in mid-set pick the lifter up at the right rep; the strength window's dots and `rep` sound land at each rep's far point (`repPeakAt`). Cardio uses the view's `speed` as before.
  - You on a machine (`client/gym.ts`): while your window's open and the office has you on the station, `player.rig` holds you at the machine's spot; the camera is the machine's (`gym.zoomed`, `gym.showsYou` in `features/places` hide the first-person hands and show your body); closing the window puts you down at its `off` spot. The cardio and strength windows dock along the bottom over a light veil (`ui/gym/gym.css`).
  - Hooks in upstream files: `src/client/world/character/person.ts` (`Bones`, `Person.setWorkout`, `Person.bones`, one line at the end of `update`; `Bones` exported from `world/character/index.ts`), `src/client/core/loop.ts` (`parts.places.seesYou()`: your body shown or hidden); `src/client/sound/index.ts` (`gymAt`: footfalls, flywheel whirr, the rower's whoosh, a stack's clank, a bar's thud, a punch, from where the machine stands; the recipes in `sound/gym.ts`). The `GymPlace` host's `people`/`you`/`now`/`camera`/`soundAt` and `gym.zoomed` (no hands drawn) are in `src/client/features/places/index.ts`.

#### Rooms, spa and detail

Inside, the gym is a whole club, not just its machines: a rubber floor zoned per kind of training (a cardio strip, square free-weight tiles with lifting platforms under the deadlift and the squat rack, a lilac stretch area with mats, foam rollers and a ball, a functional turf lane along the south wall with a push sled, a tractor tyre, plyo boxes, a kettlebell rack and battle ropes), big windows onto the street, a ceiling of steel beams, two ducts, a cable tray, pendant lamps and a slow big fan, a lobby with reception, the logo wall and turnstiles whose glass flaps swing open as someone comes through, lockers with a bench and the door to the changing room, the juice bar (a back bar with jars and a drinks fridge, the menu board with the smoothies, blender, fruit bowls, cups, five stools you can sit on), a water fountain, a towel rack, plate and medicine-ball racks, mirrors, posters, wall speakers, two TVs over the cardio deck playing a workout loop (a canvas, 12 fps) and the class timetable. The wellness spa is walled off in wood in the south-east corner: a Finnish sauna and a steam room you **walk into** through a glass door that swings open as anyone comes up (no collider in the doorway), tiered cedar benches and tiled benches you sit on, the stove with glowing stones, bucket and ladle, hourglass and thermometer, warm (or cool) light inside, a heat shimmer and the steam room's standing mist; a jacuzzi with steps, rippling water and bubbles (more with someone in), an open cold plunge with bobbing ice, four loungers with towels, a massage room behind a curtain with two low tables and a masseur by each, candles, plants and a quiet ambience of its own. When you're inside a cabin and your camera isn't (third person), its walls and roof lift away so you can see in.

Through the door in the west wall by the lockers is the **changing room**, a room of its own built onto the hall's outside (the interior is its own scene, so there's room out there): the door swings open as someone comes up, lockers along its west wall (one standing open with a towel in it), a bench in the middle you can sit on, sinks under a long mirror, a hair dryer, hooks with towels, a scale, and three tiled showers along the back wall that run (water and a little steam) while someone stands under one. In there the camera keeps to the changing room.

- **Walk-in rooms (server-checked):** the sauna and the steam room aren't sat at with `gym.sit` any more (it's refused, "Walk in through the glass door"). The server puts you on them while it sees you inside (`Gym.moved`, called from the `move` and `sit` cases whenever the peer is in the gym, reads the position the office already keeps; a little give at the door: in 0.25 m through it, out 0.2 m past it), takes you off when you walk out (unless another tab of yours is still in there), and a stray `gym.stand` doesn't. Recovery runs as before, ×1.25 while you sit on one of the room's benches (a peer's `seat`, only accepted in the gym), ×1.5 for 40 s after an **Aufguss**.
- **Aufguss:** E at the sauna's bucket (or the steam room's eucalyptus bowl) sends the existing `gym.act {station, action: 'ladle'}` (an `Interactable.gymAct`, no window). Only from inside, one per room every 30 s whoever pours ("The stones are still hissing · next Aufguss in 12 s"), the gym's per-person rate limit as well. Everyone inside gets a little energy and a toast, the pourer the XP; the station's `puffAt` goes to everyone in the gym, so everyone sees the burst of steam, and whoever is in the spa hears the hiss. **No new message types**, nothing new to sort in `guests.ts` or `party.ts`; `WellnessView` has two new optional fields (`puffBy`, `walkIn`).
- **Getting in (the jacuzzi, the plunge, the massage tables):** E there sits you down in the jacuzzi's water (one of its four places round the tub, facing the middle, arms along the rim), crouches you neck-deep and shivering in the plunge's ice, or lays you face down on a massage table while the masseur beside it kneads your back; the masseurs stand by with their hands folded otherwise. Like a machine: the office has you in it (the same `gym.sit`/`gym.stand`), the page holds you at your place and frames the camera, ✕ or Esc gets you out down the jacuzzi's steps, beside the plunge or off the foot of the table. Which place you're in is the office's (`WellnessView.slots`, a new optional field: who's in each place, '' for empty; you keep your place when someone else gets out), so every page poses everyone in the same one. **No new message types.**
- Own files:
  - `src/shared/gym-changing.ts`: the changing room (`CHANGING_ROOM`, its door and doorway, lockers, bench, sinks, showers, `showerAt`, `inChanging`, `CHANGING_SEATING`, `changingFixtures()`: the hall's west wall either side of the door, the room's walls, floor and ceiling, what stands in it).
  - `src/client/world/gym/changing.ts` (the changing room, its door and showers), `src/client/world/gym/soak.ts` (the open cold plunge, the masseurs, posing whoever's in the jacuzzi, the plunge or on a table, where you're held and step out, the camera there).
  - `src/shared/gym-rooms.ts`: the plan: the spa, the cabins (`SAUNA`, `STEAM`, `WALK_INS`, `walkInAt`, `cabinWalls`), the Aufguss's numbers and maths (`aufgussWait`, `walkInFactor`, `onBenchIn`), where the jacuzzi, plunge, massage room, loungers, reception, turnstiles, lockers, juice bar, stretch area and turf lane stand, `gymFixtures()` (every solid thing besides the machines and the room's walls: the page's colliders), and `GYM_SEATING` (sauna and steam benches, loungers, bar stools, three benches; `gym: true`); the places you get into (`isSoak`, `soakPlace`, `soakOff`, `JACUZZI_WATER`, `PLUNGE_WATER`, `MASSAGE_TOP`, `MASSEURS`).
  - `src/client/world/gym/rooms.ts` (the hall and everything in it but the machines and the spa), `spa.ts` (the spa, the cabins, their doors, clouds and lights), `kit.ts` (shared shorthands: plain pieces go into one group merged by colour at the end, `seatable`, plants, speakers, candles), `textures.ts` (the canvases: floors, planks, tiles, turf, the window view, posters, the timetable, the menu, lockers, water, `WorkoutVideo`), `particles.ts` (`Cloud`: soft particles in one `THREE.Points`, no raycasts).
  - `tests/gym-rooms.test.ts`: nothing stands on a machine, the door and the entrance stay walkable (a grid walk from the door to the sauna, the steam room, the spa, the juice bar, every machine), the cabins are closed but for their doors, every seat is in the gym only and can be got up from, the door's give, the Aufguss's cooldown and boost, and the server's walk-in, bench bonus, Aufguss and leaving; the changing room only through its door, its showers and bench reachable; the jacuzzi's, plunge's and tables' places and room to get out, and the office keeping who's in which place.
- Hooks: `world/gym/interior.ts` calls `buildGymRooms`/`buildGymSpa`, adds `gymFixtures()` as colliders, routes wellness and juice-bar states to them, and `update(t, dt, view)` takes you, your camera and everyone in the gym (the cardio and strength builders are untouched); `client/gym.ts`: `gymAct` in `use` and `hint`, the walk-in line in the HUD, the hiss, the spa's ambience, being held in the jacuzzi, plunge or on a table like on a machine (`riding`, with `soak.spot`/`off`/`frame`), the camera's room in the changing room; `world/gym/rooms.ts`: the west wall built either side of the changing room's door (its colliders are `changingFixtures`); `ui/gym/registry.ts`, `sound/gym.ts` (`gym('hiss')`, `GymSpa`) and `sound/index.ts` (`gymSpa(level)`); `shared/gym.ts`: the wellness stations and the juice bar moved into the new rooms (the cardio and strength stations stay where they were); `server/gym/*`: `GymPlayer.where`, `Gym.moved`, `GymContext.seatKey`, the walk-in wellness; `src/server/fork/office.ts` (`where` in `gymPlayer`); `src/shared/layout.ts` (`SeatDef.gym`, `...GYM_SEATING`, `seatHere(..., inGym)`), `src/shared/maps/index.ts` (`seatHereOn(..., inGym)`), `src/server/ws/handlers/presence.ts` (`gym.moved` in `move` and `sit`, `sit` passes `inGym`), `src/client/world/types.ts` (`Interactable.gymAct`), `src/client/features/places/index.ts` (the gym host's `camera`, `people`, `ambience`).
- Fog: the gym is a place of its own; its `mood` pushes the fog out to 400–500 m and the haze's far term starts at 135 m, so nothing in the 28 × 20 m hall (or its cabins) is ever hazed.

#### The basement and Gym FM

**Gym FM:** the gym plays a live radio stream through its speakers, one station for everyone inside (`GYM_CHANNELS` in `src/shared/gym-radio.ts`: I♥WORKOUT, I♥HARDSTYLE, I♥MAINSTAGE, I♥BASS from ilovemusic.de, all https, and off). The sound system on the reception counter is a station of its own (`GYM_RADIO`, kind `radio`, `server/gym/radio.ts`): E there opens a picker (`ui/gym/radio.ts`, no `gym.sit`): a card per station sends `gym.act {station: 'radio', action: 'tune', data: {channel}}` (built-in channel ids only, so no URLs from pages; 1.5 s apart whoever picks), the speakers' slider sends `volume` (0–200 % for everyone, on top of each person's jukebox volume; throttled to ~3 a second), without sitting down (`GymGame.walkUp`). Station and volume are kept in `gym-radio.json` in the data folder. Its view (`GymRadioView`) reaches everyone in the gym like any station's. The page plays it as an `<audio>` element (`client/sound/gymradio.ts`, `sound.gymRadio`) at your music volume: all of it on the gym floor, 0.3 in the changing room, nothing in the wellness spa or the basement (quiet is the point there); after a few silent seconds it lets go of the stream. **No new message types.**

**The basement:** where the spa's loungers stood, a stair goes down through a hole in the gym's floor (`STAIR`, `STAIRWELL`, a glass balustrade round it; the spa's towel shelf moved to the west wall past it, `TOWEL_SHELF`) to a floor 4.2 m below (`BASEMENT_FLOOR`). The gym's street (the floor of nothing, `player.street`) is `GYM_UNDER` (−9) while you're in the gym, so the pools can go deeper than a street (`core/travel.ts`, `syncStack`). Down there: the foyer at the stair's foot; the **quiet room** (loungers and water beds under a starry ceiling, an aquarium, dim light: the gym's `mood` dims the hemi and ambient there); the **salt grotto** (glowing salt-brick walls, a graduation wall, warm light; E at the brine pump is an Aufguss of salt mist); the **Kneipp room** (a cold tread basin one step down, three adventure showers that run while anyone stands under them, the storm with lightning and thunder); and out past the gym's south wall a tall **pool hall**: the 25 m **lap pool** (four lanes, ropes, starting blocks, backstroke flags, ladders, a pace clock, loungers, a lifeguard's chair) and the **whirlpool grotto** (a cave of rock with a waterfall, a waist-deep bubbling basin with steps and a bench). In its south wall the passage to the **thermal baths** (`THERME_PASSAGE`) ends at a glass door through to them (the tunnel's walls are x 18..22, the door at z 87.4, the floor the basement's; see "The thermal baths" below).

- **Who's where (server-checked):** the salt grotto, the quiet room, the whirlpool, the Kneipp basin and the lap pool are walk-in wellness stations (`salt`, `rest`, `grotto`, `kneipp`, `lappool` in `GYM_STATIONS`, their spots in `WELLNESS_SPOTS`), like the sauna: `GymPlayer.where` now carries `y`, and `walkInAt(x, z, margin, y)` / `stillIn` go by how far down you are (feet below `BASEMENT_SPLIT` are downstairs), because the basement's rooms are under the spa's (the Kneipp basin is under the steam room). `WALK_IN_BY_STATION` holds both kinds (`AnyWalkIn`). The Kneipp walk gives a cold burst on the way in, swimming earns the most XP, the quiet room gives energy back fastest.
- **Swimming** (`client/gym-pool.ts`, `GymPool`, the one swim controller `client/swim/` with the lap pool's data `LAP_SWIM`, see "Swimming" below): over the lap pool and below its surface the pool holds you (`player.rig`), like the roof's pool; the deck's colliders reach down past a swimmer's feet, so its edge keeps you in; E at a wall climbs out (`lapClimbOut`, never onto a block); E at the water from the deck dives in. Wall to wall is a timed length (a toast, your best in `localStorage`). Everyone in the pool is posed swimming (`seaPose`) from where they are. Its keys and hint are an activity (`gympool`) in `features/places/index.ts`.
- **The camera** keeps to the basement room you're in (`Room.vault`, set per room by `GymPlace`), so it never ends up in a wall between two.
- Own files: `src/shared/gym-basement.ts` (the plan: floor, stair, rooms, pools, showers, seats, the walk-in rooms, `basementFixtures()` and `gymFloorSlabs()`, `cutOut`), `src/shared/gym-radio.ts`, `src/server/gym/radio.ts`, `src/client/gym-pool.ts`, `src/client/sound/gymradio.ts`, `src/client/world/gym/basement/` (`index.ts`, `shell.ts` floors/walls/ceilings/stair/foyer, `rooms.ts` quiet room/salt grotto/Kneipp, `hall.ts` pool hall/lap pool/grotto/baths passage, `textures.ts`), `src/client/world/gym/radio.ts`; `tests/gym-basement.test.ts` (the stair a step at a time inside its hole, the gym's floor everywhere else, everything down there walkable from the stair's foot with heights, every seat can be got up from, the rooms know they're down there, climbing out of the pool, the server's walk-in by height, Gym FM for everyone with its cooldown and only built-in stations).
- Hooks: `shared/gym-rooms.ts` (the loungers went, the stair's rail in `gymFixtures`, `walkInAt`'s `y`, `stillIn`, `AnyWalkIn`, `gymSeats()`), `shared/layout.ts` (`...gymSeats()` instead of `...GYM_SEATING`), `shared/gym.ts` (the five basement stations, `GymKind` `radio`), `shared/gym-wellness.ts` (their spots), `server/gym/index.ts` (registers Gym FM, `walkUp`, the walk-in by height), `server/gym/game.ts` (`walkUp`), `server/gym/wellness.ts` (`salt mist`), `server/fork/office.ts` (`y` in `where`), `client/core/travel.ts` (`GYM_UNDER` in the gym), `client/gym.ts` (the vault, `within` and `arrived` know the basement, the radio's reach, the ambience and light down there, the pool, the hints), `client/features/places/index.ts` (the host's `radio`/`thunder`, `y` in `spaPeople`, the `gympool` activity), `client/sound/index.ts` (`gymRadio`), `client/sound/gym.ts` (`thunder`), `client/world/gym/interior.ts` (builds the basement and the radio, routes their states, `walkInAt(x, z, y)`), `client/world/gym/rooms.ts` and `spa.ts` (the floors open over the stair: `decalHoled` in `kit.ts`; `gymFloorSlabs()` instead of one floor collider).

### The thermal baths

Behind the gym, through the glass door at the end of its basement's pool hall: the **Thermenwelt Flogge** (the name is `THERME_NAME` in `src/shared/therme.ts`, one place to change it), a place of its own like the gym and the Schallwerk (`THERME = '@therme'` is your `floor` while you're in there). Modelled on a big German thermal bath in spirit only: none of a real one's names. It is built in phases, one pull request each, by the plan in `~/cloude_code/therme-bauplan.md`; **phase 0** is the empty house and the way in.

- **The plan** (`src/shared/therme.ts`, its own coordinates, the floor at y 0; it shares nothing with the gym but the door): the house is `THERME_BOX` x 0..200, z 0..140; the north band (z 0..15) has the passage from the gym (`ZONES.gang`, x 96..104) and the main entrance (`ZONES.lobby`, for when the baths get a house of their own on the street); south of it side by side the Saunadorf (`dorf`, x 0..55), the Thermenparadies under the dome (`paradies`, x 55..140, with the wave pool `WELLENBAD` in its south half) and the Rutschenwelt (`rutschen`, x 140..200); out past the south wall the lagoon (`lagune`, z 140..190). `ROOFS` (the band 6 m, the eaves round the dome 12 m, the slide hall 34 m; the Saunadorf has none, it's a garden under the sky inside a `DORF_FENCE` of 2.8 m), `DOME` (an ellipse on the eaves rising to 28 m; `domeHeight`), `DOORS` (each opening in a wall; the `shut` ones are hoarded up until their part is built: the entrance, the Saunadorf, the lagoon), `thermeWalls()` and `thermeFixtures()` (every solid thing: the page's colliders, the tests' walls), `THERME_ARRIVAL` and `GYM_FROM_THERME` (either side of the door), `zoneAt`, `thermeWhereabouts`.
- **The door** (`client/therme/place.ts`, `ThermePlace`): the gym's basement passage has the kind `therme` on its glass, stile and push bars (`world/gym/basement/hall.ts`); **E** there sends `floor.go` to `THERME`, **E** at the door at the passage's north end sends `floor.go` to `GYM`. The office lands you by the door on either side (`thermeDoorSpot` in `server/therme/place.ts`, from the floor you're leaving), and so does the page (a place places you itself): the baths' `arrived` runs after the gym's, so going back it has the last word and puts you in the gym's passage, not at its front door. **No new message types**, nothing new for `guests.ts` or `party.ts`: guests and party guests go in like anyone.
- **Inside** (`client/world/therme/`, built the first time anyone goes in, hidden when you leave): `shell.ts` the floor (sandstone tiles, pale blue in the Rutschenwelt), the walls with a sandstone skirting, the ceilings, the slide hall's trusses, the eaves with the dome's ellipse cut out, and the dome (glass, `DoubleSide`, no depth write, on 24 steel ribs, five rings and a ring beam, all one merged mesh); `gang.ts` the tiled passage with its light strips, the door back, the name over the passage's mouth, the hoardings with what's coming behind them, stand signs where each part comes, the wave pool's outline painted in dashes; `kit.ts` the shorthands (plain toon pieces go in `still`, merged by colour once; anything textured or see-through as it is). The sky shows through the dome: the light follows it (`mood`: warm sun by day, the hall's own warm light at night, the fog pushed out past the hall).
- **Two fixes this needed elsewhere:** going straight from one place to another (the gym and the baths) used to let the place you left hand you the office's walls after the new one had given you its own, so you fell through its floor; `setPlace` in `features/places/index.ts` now lets every place you left go first. And a reload used to put you back at most 60 m from the middle (`arrivalSpot` in `office/input.ts`, a floor's bounds): places that are bigger have their own bounds (`PLACE_BOUNDS`/`placeSpotFrom` in `server/fork/office.ts`): the baths all over, the gym down to its basement's passage at z 88.
- **Phase 2, the Thermenparadies** (`src/shared/therme-paradies.ts`, the plan of the dome's north half; the wave pool takes the south half): the **thermal pool** (34 °C), a ring of water round the **palm island** (freeform: several rectangles, `THERMAL_POOL`), with a notch under the **cliff** where the **waterfall** comes down; the **swim-up bar** in its east arm (`BAR_COUNTER` standing in the water, five stools in it, `BAR_FLOOR` behind with the back bar, a thatched roof and Kai the bartender, who turns to whoever swims up): **E** at the counter, or swimming up to it, opens its menu (`SWIMBAR_MENU`, the roof's drinks, served into your hand like the Schallwerk's); four **whirlpools** (36 °C, bubbling) along the west deck with bamboo between them, **bubble ledges** along the pool's north wall; the **dripstone grotto** in the north-west corner (rock walls open to the east, stalactites and stalagmites, glowing crystals, a light slowly changing colour (the Paradies's one point light), steam over its warm basin); 30 **loungers** along the decks and on the island (kind `thermeseat`: **E** lies you down like the roof's and the gym's, `liePose`; as the Schallwerk's sofas nobody tells the office, every page lays down whoever stands still on one, `client/therme/loungers.ts`); 15 **palms** (instanced, their crowns swaying) in planters and on the island. Every pool is a `PoolDef` (`shared/swim.ts`), so swimming is the one controller's (`ThermePlace.swim`, the `thermeswim` activity in `features/places`: **W A S D**, **Shift**, **Space**, **E** climbs out, or orders at the bar). `shared/therme-all.ts` puts the house and its parts together (`THERME_POOLS`, `thermeFixtures()` with the floor open over the water and its slabs down past a swimmer's feet, `thermeWhereabouts`: im Thermalbecken, an der Schwimmbar, im Whirlpool, in der Tropfsteingrotte, auf der Palmeninsel). The client: `world/therme/paradies.ts` (each pool's water, mosaic basin and coping as one mesh each, laid by world position: `rectsGeometry`/`edgeWallsGeometry` in `kit.ts`; the island, the bar, the loungers, the bubbles and the waterfall), `grotto.ts`, `palms.ts`. In `shared/swim.ts`: `poolEdges` (a pool's walls: its rectangles' sides, less the seams) and `climbOutWays` (the page climbs out by the nearest wall with room behind it, not into a rock or the counter). `tests/therme-paradies.test.ts`.
- **Phase 3, the wave pool** (`src/shared/therme-waves.ts`): the dome's south half (`WELLENBAD`); a **beach** at its north end stepping down from the deck into the water (`beachSteps()`: twelve 10 cm steps, walked like stairs, wading most of the way) and the **deep water** beyond (`DEEP`, `WAVE_POOL`, a `PoolDef` with `swell` and `flow`). The **waves** run by the office's clock (`store.officeNow()`), so every page has the same sea at the same moment and nothing goes over the wire: every 8 minutes (`WAVES.every`) for 2 minutes (`WAVES.run`), easing in and out over 8 s, 45 cm high, 9 m from crest to crest, rolling toward the beach and a little slanted (`waveStrength`, `waveSwell`, `nextWaves`, `wavesLeft`); swimmers ride up and down with the surface and are carried shoreward (`waveFlow`), held off the beach by its last step unless a wave washes them up onto it, where they're back on their feet. A horn as each run starts (the soccer hall's air horn, `soccerCrowd('horn')`; `sound/index.ts` is at its size limit), the board over the beach (`WAVE_BOARD`, `waveBoard`: Wellen in 3:12, or 🌊 WELLEN! noch 1:30), foam where they break. The page (`world/therme/waves.ts`): the steps in sand tiles, the basin in mosaic, the surface as a 60 × 44 grid lifted each frame while there are waves and you're near. `tests/therme-waves.test.ts`.
- **Phase 4, the Rutschenwelt** (`src/shared/therme-slides.ts`): the **slide tower** (`TOWER`, red legs, three yellow platforms at 9, 17 and 25 m round the blue lift shaft `LIFT_CORE`, railings open where each slide goes off, a red canopy at 28.5 m), the **lift** (kind `thermelift`, a door on the ground and on each platform: **E** opens its buttons, `client/therme/slide-ui.ts` `openLift`), seven **slides** fanning out from it so none comes near another (`SLIDES`: their paths as points, `helix`/`loop` for the black hole's spiral and the looping; `lanePath` for the racer's four lanes): 🕳️ Schwarzes Loch (a dark tube, rings of colour flashing inside), ⚡ Turbo (an open chute, 25 m nearly straight down), 🚪 Falltür (a glass capsule at the top: **E**, a 3-2-1 countdown, the floor drops), 🌀 Looping, 🛟 Reifenrutsche, 🌊 Wellenrutsche, 🏁 Racer (four lanes side by side). They end in the **landing pool** (`LANDING_POOL`), where the swimming takes over. **Riding** (`client/therme/slides.ts`, `SlideRider`; the `thermeslide` activity in `features/places`): **E** at a slide's gate (kind `thermeslide`, its `thermeSlide`/`thermeLane` on the `Interactable`, augmented in `world/therme/slides.ts`), gravity along the curve less the water's slip and the air's drag (so the Turbo hits 60 km/h and the Wellenrutsche ambles), the camera along it, the hint saying how long and how fast. **The office clocks every ride** (`server/therme/index.ts`, `Therme`; `therme.slide {slide, phase: 'start' | 'finish', lane?}` in `shared/therme-msgs.ts`, handled in `ws/handlers/therme.ts`): from the start it hears to the finish, faster than 22 m/s on average (`minRideMs`) or slower than 90 s is no ride; each slide's board is everyone's best, one row a person, ten shown (`therme.slides {boards}` to everyone in the baths, on arriving and when it changes; `therme.ride` to the rider: time, place, best), kept in `therme.json` (0600). The **board** on two posts by the landing pool shows each slide's best three; the **kiosk** in front of it (kind `thermeboard`) opens every board and your last **ride photo** (taken by a camera beside the slide just before its end, drawn from a render target with you in it, to save). Where the slides run low over the deck they stand in the way (`slideBlockers`), and their stilts reach down to the ground or the pool's floor. Guests and party guests may ride (`therme.slide` in `GUEST`; `therme.slides`, `therme.ride` in `PARTY_SEES`). `tests/therme-slides.test.ts` (every slide from its platform to the water, none near another nor itself a turn below, nothing in their way, every gate walked to from the lift, the office's clock and boards over a restart).
- **Phase 5, the Saunadorf** (`src/shared/therme-dorf.ts`): through the door from the Thermenparadies (open now), a village of huts on a wooden boardwalk round a **cold pond** with a jetty and reeds (`POND_POOL`, 18 °C): the **Aufgussarena** (four tiers along three walls, the stove on its stage), a **Finnish sauna**, a **Kelo** log sauna, an **earth sauna** under a grass mound, a **salt sauna** in glowing salt bricks, a **Bio-Sauna** with herbs hung up, a tiled **steam bath**; the **plunge pool**, the **ice fountain**, the **Kneipp trough** (ankle-deep, walked through), the **Ruhehaus** with its fireplace and loungers. Each sauna is `SaunaDef` (box, door, height, tiers, which walls its benches run along, its look); `dorfFixtures()` are their walls (a door gap with a lintel), roofs, benches tier by tier (`benches`), stoves; `dorfSeats()` a seat on every tier every metre or so and the Ruhehaus's loungers (kind `thermeseat`: **E** sits you on a bench or lays you on a lounger; `client/therme/loungers.ts`, now every seat of the baths, sitting or lying, at its height). **The Aufguss plan** runs by the office's clock (`aufgussAt`, `aufgussPlan`): every 5 minutes (`AUFGUSS_EVERY`; the plan's "15 office minutes" would be 37 s at an office day an hour) one sauna has one for 3 minutes, the arena every other time (`AUFGUSS_ORDER`): steam billows off its stones, the **Saunameister** stands by the stove wheeling his towel, the board at the way in (`AUFGUSS_BOARD`, `client/therme/dorf-ui.ts`) lists the next four, and a word goes round as it starts. **The office gives the bonus** (`Therme.aufguss`, looked at every 3 s from `startFork`): whoever's inside that sauna in its first 90 s gets the gym's energy back once (`AUFGUSS_BONUS`: +30 energy, +20 fitness points, into `gym.json` via `ctx.gym.fitness`) and a `therme.aufguss` message. While you're in a hut and your camera isn't (third person), its walls, roof, sign and door lift away (`Dorf.shells`). Quiet in there: no music. The page: `world/therme/dorf.ts`. `tests/therme-dorf.test.ts`.
- **Phase 6, the outdoor lagoon** (`src/shared/therme-lagune.ts`): through the glass doors in the Thermenparadies's south wall (open now), outside under the open sky (z 140..190, a hedge round it, trees beyond): a sandy **beach** along the house with 20 loungers under sunshades, the warm **lagoon** (freeform, `LAGOON`) to the west, and to the east the **lazy river** (`RIVER`): a channel round an island whose current (`riverFlow`, a `PoolDef.flow`) carries you clockwise round and round at 0.95 m/s, under the **footbridge** onto the island (its underside over a swimmer's head, a step up at each end, rails), palms and six more loungers on the island. It's outdoors (`outside`): out there the page tells the sky it isn't indoors, so the office's real weather falls (rain, snow) and the sky lights it as it is; back under the dome it's the dome's light again. On cold days (`store.sky.temp` under 10 °C) steam rises off the warm water, more the colder it is. The page: `world/therme/lagune.ts` (the river's arms each their own surface, its ripples running with the current); the loungers are seats like the rest (`client/therme/loungers.ts`). `tests/therme-lagune.test.ts` (outside and apart, the current clockwise everywhere, a swimmer let go carried right round, every lounger and the island walked to over the bridge, swimmers under it).
- **Phase 7, the house on the street** (`src/shared/therme-street.ts`): south of the gym, where the farm's long field ran (`FARM.fields[0]` in `shared/scenic.ts` now ends at z 86 instead of 104), its lot `THERME_STREET_BOX` x 36..133, z 90..138, its front and **main entrance** on the side street to the west (x 28, paved from z 80 to 140): a long cream hall (`THERME_STREET_HALL`) with bands of glass lit warm at night, palms behind the west front's glass, the glass **dome** on its ribs over the roof's middle (`STREET_DOME`), the **slide tower** free-standing east of the hall with three tubes corkscrewing round it (`STREET_TOWER`), its name along the roof's west edge, the entrance under a canopy with glass doors that slide apart (`world/therme/exterior.ts`, the `thermeOut` fixture in the street's outlook, so it's there from every floor's street, the windows and the roof; one collider for the hall, one for the tower). The house outside is a likeness, not the inside's size. **E** at its doors (kind `thermestreet`) goes in to the **entrance hall** inside (the `lobby` zone, open now, `world/therme/lobby.ts`: the box office with Lena the cashier, turnstiles whose arms turn as you pass, lockers, the info board with the next waves and Aufgüsse, its glass doors in the north wall: **E** there goes back out onto your floor's street in front of the house). The office lands you at the box office coming off a floor and by the gym's door coming from the gym (`thermeDoorSpot`). The street furniture keeps off the lot (`built` in `shared/streetside.ts`), the golf balls and the loop's trees too (`neighbourBoxes` in `world/outside.ts`), DER BRECHER's keep-outs have it (`shared/coaster-keepout.ts`); the minimap draws the hall, the dome, the tower and the doors (`features/minimap/atlas.ts`) and has 🌴 Thermenwelt at the doors, people in the baths shown at the house (`pois.ts`). `tests/therme-street.test.ts` (clear of the gym, the Schallwerk, the halls, the casino, the farm, the town's buildings, the roads and the loop; the doors face the road; on the map; in at the box office, through the turnstiles).
- **Phase 8, life and sound**: thirty **other bathers** (`src/shared/therme-bathers.ts` `batherAt`: where each is by the office's clock, so every page sees the same people in the same places, nothing on the wire; `client/therme/bathers.ts` draws them): nine doing lengths round the palm island, five riding the waves (lifted by the same swell), four in the whirlpools, six lying on loungers (those loungers are taken, `NPC_LOUNGERS`), four walking round the thermal pool's deck, two floating round the lazy river; only the nearest fourteen within 60 m are drawn and moved. **The sound** (`client/therme/sound.ts`, `ThermeSounds` on the office's audio, `ctx.sound.core`: the one line `sound/index.ts` gives a part that makes its own sounds, the file at its 600): the hall's air (a low rumble, water, voices washing round with a little echo; how much by where you are: all of it under the dome and by the slides, half in the passage and the entrance hall, a hush in the Saunadorf, softer out at the lagoon; the voices more the more people are near), a whoop from whoever goes down a slide (you too), a splash as someone lands in the landing pool; the waves' horn and the Aufguss as before. **A performance round**: textured pieces sharing a material merged with their UVs kept (`mergeTextured` in `world/therme/kit.ts`: the grotto's rock and crystals, each hut's walls and roof, the cliff, the lanterns), the black hole's rings one mesh a colour; in the test browser 4–5 ms a frame in every part, 60–355 draw calls, 60–140k triangles (from up to 950 and 690k). In `shared/swim.ts`, a point exactly on the seam of two rectangles is water (a swimmer carried along one no longer drops out there). `tests/therme-life.test.ts` (thirty, by the clock, over a whole day every swimmer in the water and clear of everything, the walkers clear of everything).
- **The finishing round** (after the eight phases, from Florian's walk through it):
  - **Down a tube you're inside it.** Every closed slide (the black hole, the looping, the Falltür's) and the Turbo is ridden lying on your back, feet first, on the tube's floor and square to it wherever it goes, upside down through the loop (`RIDE_POSE` in `shared/therme-slides.ts`; `client/therme/slide-pose.ts` `SlidePoser`); on the open ones you sit (Wellenrutsche), ride a double tyre (Reifenrutsche) or a mat belly down, head first (racer, a mat a lane's colour). The camera rides inside: your eyes (a little behind your hips, the mouse turning your head a little) or, in third person, behind and over you. The others on a slide are posed the same, found by the nearest point of a track's floor (every track sampled once). The tubes are see-through coloured plastic now, a flange every 2 m, LED rings every 2.5 m; open troughs have rounded rims. The track's frames (`slideFrames`/`frameAt` in `world/therme/slides.ts`, carried along without twisting) are shared by the geometry, the rider and the poses. **The ride photo** on a closed slide is taken inside the tube, from under its roof ahead of your feet, looking back along you to your face, the tube lit up for the flash, a vignette round it.
  - **The other bathers keep to dry floor.** They walk routes now (`ROUTES` in `shared/therme-bathers.ts`, there and back): the thermal pool's decks, the sauna garden's gravel paths, the lagoon's beach; the old round of the thermal pool walked across the waterfall's notch. Thirty-eight of them: swimmers across the lagoon, two in the garden's hot tub, one at the swim-up bar, two on the garden's lawn loungers (`NPC_LAWN`). `tests/therme-life.test.ts` walks every bit of every route (no foot over any water, nothing in the way) and keeps every swimmer a body's width inside its pool.
  - **Every pool says what it is.** `src/shared/therme-pools.ts`: `POOL_INFO` (name, °C, what's special) for every pool, `depthOf`, `signWords`, and `POOL_SIGNS`: a sign on the deck by each (its post solid, read from both sides; the grotto's over its mouth), the Kneipp trough too; the swim hint uses the same info. And every pool has the same finish (`world/therme/decor.ts`): a rounded stone coping with the overflow gutter's grate behind it, lights in its walls under the water, the light the water throws dancing on its floor (one additive mesh for all of them), steel ladders, its depth on the coping; the pond and the lagoon are natural, boulders along their banks instead. **Showers** by the ways in (`SHOWERS`, kind `thermeuse`): **E** under one rinses you.
  - **The Saunadorf is a garden under the open sky**, fenced: no roof over it (`ROOFS` has none for it), the west and south walls a larch fence on posts (`DORF_FENCE`), the north band's face to it clad in wood; lawn, gravel paths between the huts (`DORF_PATHS`), wooden porches before the doors, pines and birches (`DORF_TREES`, solid trunks), a meadow and a forest beyond the fence, lanterns; the huts got chimneys (smoke where wood burns), lit windows. It's outdoors (`underSky` in `shared/therme-all.ts`: the garden and the lagoon), so the weather falls there too; still quiet. **Every water in it is for using**: the pond (natural now: a stony bed, green water, reeds, bulrushes, water lilies) and the plunge pool to swim in, a new **hot tub** on the lawn (`GARDEN_TUB`/`TUB_POOL`, 38 °C, bubbling, steaming on cold days, a cedar deck round it), the **Kneipp trough** waded through (a slosh a step, a word going in and another once you've waded its length, a handrail along it), the **ice fountain** and two **gush buckets** on their frame against the house (kind `thermeuse`: **E** and a bucketful of cold water comes down on you). A fire pit with six logs round it to sit on, sixteen loungers on the lawn. `client/therme/dorf-life.ts` (`DorfLife`) has the Saunadorf's goings-on (the Aufguss, the huts' walls, the boards, the Kneipp trough) and the things to use; the page: `world/therme/garden.ts`, `dorf.ts`.
  - **The dome hall's west and south walls are glass** (`world/therme/facade.ts`, still solid in the plan): from the pools you look out at the garden and the lagoon, from outside in at the palms. Along its north wall a **mural** of a tropical shore at sunset, lamps, and a **clock** over the passage showing the time.
  - **More that stands about** (`src/shared/therme-furniture.ts`, solid, clear of every door, path and pool): tropical **planting beds** (`PLANT_BEDS`, planted by `world/therme/plants.ts` `Planting`: ferns, monstera, banana plants, flowering shrubs, all one mesh a colour) along the walls, round the wave pool, on the palm island and outside along the house; two **lifeguards** in high chairs (`LIFEGUARDS`) by the wave pool and the thermal pool; the **beach bar** at the lagoon (`STRANDBAR`, Lina behind the counter; **E** orders like the swim-up bar). And (`world/therme/details.ts`) little tables with drinks between the loungers, lights strung under the swim-up bar's thatch and its menu, torches on the islands, the slide hall's supergraphic, a light by every slide's gate (red while you're on that slide); in the wave pool the machine's grilles in the deep end's wall and a line of floats where the beach drops away, riding the waves.
  - **Why the pools' walls looked grey from the water:** the toon outline is a hull pushed off each mesh along its normals; off a pool's wall it lay between you and the wall and covered it. Flat sheets (`rectsGeometry`, `edgeWallsGeometry`) carry `noOutline` on their geometry now, and the walls glow a little more (lit from above only, through the water). In the test browser 5.6–7.4 ms a frame, up to 389 draw calls and 380k triangles. `tests/therme-detail.test.ts` (every pool named on a sign by it; signs, showers, beds and furniture on dry floor, out of the water and every way through; the showers, buckets, ice, hot tub, Kneipp trough and beach bar walked to; the garden under the sky and fenced, every water in it swum in or waded; the tubes ridden lying).
  - **Light outside, and how a sauna feels** (afterwards): out in the sauna garden and at the lagoon the sky still lights it, but never darker than its lamps would keep it (`ThermePlace.mood`: a floor under the hemisphere and ambient light, warm after dark), and every lantern, lamp and torch has a soft halo after dark (`halos` in `ThermeParts`, one additive `Points` for all of them, `world/therme/index.ts`). In a hut (a sauna, the Ruhehaus) it's indoors: no weather, its own dim warm light (the steam bath's cool white), and a floor (duckboards, the steam bath's tiles). **In a sauna** (`client/therme/sauna-feel.ts`, `SaunaFeel`): its heat (the sauna's own, hotter the higher the tier, a surge with its Aufguss) and how damp it is (dry Finnish air … the steam bath's fog; the Aufguss pours a wave of steam) make the screen's edges glow hot and its colours warm, steam drift across it and fill the room (the scene's fog closing in, the steam bath down to a few metres, the view softly blurred), sweat bead and run down it the longer you sit; a card under the corner's name says how hot and how damp (`🌡️ 94 °C · 💧 12 %`, `🔥 Aufguss!`). Cold water washes the sweat off: the pond, the plunge pool, a gush bucket, the ice, a shower. The stove crackles and the stones hiss with each ladle of the Aufguss, the steam bath's generator puffs every few seconds (`crackle`, `hiss` in `client/therme/sound.ts`). Respects reduced motion (the overlay's drifting and throbbing stop).
