### People in the streets

Passers-by on the city's sidewalks (60 to 150 round you by day, about a third of that at night): out of a shop door, round the blocks (alone, two together chatting, or with a dog on a lead), across the zebras (the city's cars stop for them), sitting on benches, waiting at bus stops, looking into shop windows, and into another shop; umbrellas when it rains, now and then a shopping bag. Everyone sees the same people at the same places without anything on the wire: each is a slot at a crossing whose walk in each 200-second epoch comes from a seed and the office's clock (`store.officeNow`), and how many are out from how light it was then (the sun from the office's place). Only your page's: they step aside for you, other people and the garage's cars (waiting a moment and catching up when there's no room), glance at you, stumble when bumped. No colliders, so nobody gets stuck on them. Drawn as a few dozen instanced meshes (people, hair per style, dogs, bags, umbrellas, leads), only within 122 m, faces and hands within 45 m; about 0.5 ms of script a frame.

- Own files:
  - `src/shared/streetside.ts`: where the street furniture and the city's lamps stand (moved out of `world/town/furniture.ts` and `ground.ts`, same numbers in the same order), so the passers-by know the benches, stops, lamps and bollards.
  - `src/shared/sidewalks.ts`: the walkable graph: `WALKS` (each sidewalk, crossing to crossing, the office's street's too), `CORNERS` (joined by the walks, the zebras and straight on), what stands on a walk (`posts`, gone round by `detour`) and the spots beside it (benches, bus stops, shop windows and doors, `shopFaces` the same fronts `world/town/shops.ts` draws); `onRoad`. Nothing on the office's own ground (its building, the lot in front of the garage, the side lot).
  - `src/shared/passersby.ts`: `SLOTS`, `epochOf`, `planFor` (one outing, door to door), `bodyAt` (where each of the party is at a time), `density`.
  - `src/client/world/town/people.ts` (`buildPassersby`: the page's side, stepping aside, bumps, heads, walk cycle, LOD, the obstacles for the cars; `stats`, `shift` and `list()` for a look from the console), `src/client/world/town/people-models.ts` (the instanced parts and the colors).
  - `src/client/features/town/people.ts`: `drivePassersby` (the clock, the daylight, the rain, the sounds). `src/client/sound/passersby.ts`: footsteps and chatter.
  - `tests/passersby.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/client/sound/index.ts`: `passerby(kind, at)` in the fork's section.
  - Fork files from "One city round the office": `src/client/world/town/index.ts` (`Town.people`, built with the town, updated before the cars, whose obstacles get the passers-by out in the road), `src/client/features/town/index.ts` (`drivePassersby` each frame before the town's update), `src/client/world/town/furniture.ts`, `ground.ts`, `kit.ts` (laid out by `shared/streetside.ts` now).
  - `docs/features.md`: "People in the streets".

### Each storey its own cut (#20, and the balconies round the building)

No two floors of the building are laid out alike, inside or from the street. It's all worked out from the floor's place in the stack alone (`storeyPlan(index)`), the same on the server and every page, so the dog, golf, the basketball, workers walking out and the parachutes stay in step; the bottom floor is exactly as before. Every storey has the same seats by id, so seating doesn't care. Reordering the floors re-lays them.

- **Balconies on their own walls.** Each storey's furnished balcony (the bench, the table and stools, the ashtray, the tee and its bag, the sign, the lamp poles and string lights) hangs at another of five spots than the floor below's (`SPOTS` in `shared/balconies.ts`): three along the south (street) wall, one off the west wall north of the fire escape, one off the east wall between the Services board and the TV. Each spot is a stretch of wall with nothing standing against it inside; the deck reaches its own way along the wall, never past the building's corners. About half the floors also have a second, plain balcony (a deck, a railing, two plants) on another wall. The north wall (the boards, the elevator, the back office, the street behind) gets none, and no floor is without one: workers leave the upper floors off it and smoke breaks are out there.
- **One frame, turned.** A balcony is the bottom floor's turned a quarter (or not) onto its wall and slid along it: `balconyAt`/`balconyLocal`/`balconyYaw`/`balconyBox` take a point, a heading or a box between the bottom floor's balcony's coordinates and the floor's. The furniture, the tee, their colliders and what you use there are built once in that frame and turned onto each storey's (`movable`/`moveOnto` in `world/office/balcony.ts`); the decks, railings, lights (their halos are points of their own, in `NightParts.glows`) and the night lamps are laid again for every storey. The bench and stools as seats are turned by `storeySeat` wherever a seat's place is worked out (sitting down, peers sitting, walking over to someone).
- **Walls a storey at a time.** The outside walls are built again on every floor change (`storeyWalls` in `world/office/storey-walls.ts`, in place of upstream's `walls` fixture): the balconies' sliding doors, and the windows, which make way for them (and one more where the bottom floor's doors are, when that's wall). Pictures can't hang over the new doors (`Site.unwall` takes the old ones' rects down), and the rain's heard through this floor's windows (`hearThrough`).
- **Inside.** The desk pods sit a little differently, and now and then one is turned a quarter, its rows facing east and west (the nav grid, the desks' colliders and the rugs turn with them); one inside wall is painted an accent color of its own (`accentWall`), round its windows and doors; the rugs come in other colors.
- **Golf off a side balcony** is a driving range: the shot's heading is still a world heading, kept within the aim either side of straight out from that balcony (`aimWithin` in `shared/teespot.ts`), and the flight rattles round every balcony's railing and off the wall it hangs from, and lands on any balcony further down (`fly`). Parachutes go out from whichever wall (`Jump`: `dir`, `side`, `rail`).

- Fork files: `src/shared/storey.ts` (`storeyPlan`, `storeyDesks`, `storeySeat`, `balconySeatAt`, `onBalcony`), `src/shared/balconies.ts` (the spots and the frame), `src/shared/teespot.ts` (the tee per storey, the decks a ball rattles round), `src/client/world/office/storey-walls.ts`, `src/client/features/golf/storey.ts`, `src/client/world/deskgifts.ts` (the Christmas presents ride on their desks); `tests/storey.test.ts` (every storey's balconies on a wall with room for their doors, clear of the fire escape and each other, reachable through their doors round the furniture, everything on the furnished deck, the tee aimed out from it, desks in back-to-back pairs however they're turned). (Not `shared/floorplan.ts`, which is a floor's desk signs and back office.)
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/layout.ts`: `buildDesks(clusterX, pods)`, `DESK_CLUSTERS_X`, `DESK_PODS`.
  - `src/shared/nav.ts`: the office's grids per storey too (`officeNav(wing, index)`, an `index` on `walkable`, `nearestWalkable`, `route`, `wayIn`, `wayHome`, `wayToBalcony`), seats as the storey has them (`onStorey`), turned desks, and the way out through the storey's balcony doors. `src/shared/hoop.ts`: `index` on `throwOk`, `nearSolids`, `step`, `simulate`; any of the storey's balconies is in bounds.
  - Server: `src/server/floor.ts` (`FloorContext.index`, passed to the dog and the court), `src/server/office/floors.ts` (the floor's place in the open floors), `src/server/dog.ts` (`DogEnv.index`, its desks and grid), `src/server/court.ts` (the throw's bounds).
  - Client: `src/client/world/office/build.ts` (`storeyWalls` for `walls`, `Site.unwall`), `fixture.ts` (`Site.wall` hands back its rect, `unwall`), `shell.ts` (`balconyDoor(o)`), `seats.ts` (`desks` moves and turns them on `setLevel`), `balcony.ts` (laid again on `setLevel`), `room.ts` (the rugs), `src/client/world/desksigns.ts` (`setLevel`), `src/client/world/tower.ts` (each floor's balconies and doors on their walls), `src/client/world/world.ts` (the office's nav and ways, `Ways.home`'s `jump`), `src/client/world/holiday.ts` (`deskGifts`), `src/client/sound/places.ts` (`hearThrough`), `src/client/features/golf/world.ts` (`tee`, `stance(yaw, at)`, the railings and the balconies below in `fly`), `controller.ts` (`GolfHooks.spot`) and `index.ts`, `src/client/features/basketball/world.ts` + `index.ts`, `src/client/features/smoke/index.ts`, `src/client/features/walking/walkto.ts` + `index.ts`, `src/client/features/seating/index.ts`, `src/client/features/peers/index.ts`, `src/client/features/workers/leaving.ts` (the parachute), `src/client/ui/whereabouts.ts`, and desk positions from the world's desks in `src/client/features/workers/actions.ts` (`standAt`), `views.ts` and `src/client/features/palette/index.ts`.
  - Docs: `docs/features.md` (the tower, smoke breaks, golf, the bookshelf).

### Each storey its own interior

The storeys were cut their own way (above); now each is furnished its own way too. An interior (`shared/interiors.ts`, `INTERIORS`) is pure data: the floor's finish (planks, chevron parquet, polished concrete, tatami, carpet tiles, dark epoxy), the ceiling's (tiles, concrete, boards, stucco, dark), what goes on the walls (brick, light slats, Altbau wainscoting with dado and crown mouldings all round, a moss wall, seventies wallpaper over wood panelling, dark acoustic panels, shoji), the lamps over the pods and the lounge (cone, Edison bulbs in cages, paper globes, a brass chandelier, rattan, seventies domes, neon rings, paper lanterns), the rugs (pastel, round, Persian, jute, shag, neon-edged, tatami), the desks', chairs', wood's, couch's and pots' colors, the plants' size, the walls' paint over the floor's palette and an accent color of its own in place of the storey's. Eight of them: **Klassik** (the office as it always was), **Industrie-Loft**, **Skandi**, **Altbau**, **Urban Jungle**, **70er**, **Neon**, **Zen**.

- **By its place, or picked.** A floor is furnished the way its place in the stack gives it (`defaultInterior(index)`): the bottom floor Klassik, the floors above taking turns with the rest, so no two floors one over the other match. An admin can pick another for any floor in the elevator (the list beside the floor, "· auto" for its place's); the pick is kept by floor id in `<dataDir>/interiors.json` (`server/interiors.ts`, `Interiors`, 0600, only known interiors), goes out as `FloorInfo.interior`, and everyone on the floor sees the change at once. `floor.interior` is admins only (team-only for guests; party guests see `interior` in their floor list). The elevator and the floor list show every floor's interior.
- **Furnishing** (`world/office/interior/`, the `interior` fixture, last on the floor's plan): on a change of floor, palette or pick it paints the walls, trim, planks (every plank texture: the room's, the loft's, the back office's) and the ceiling tiles' canvas, swaps the materials of everything built in Klassik's desk, leg, wood, chair, couch, pot and lounge-rug colors for the interior's (collected once at build time, so workers and props added later are never touched, and a mesh someone else gave another look is left alone; the street, the tower and the balcony furniture are marked `userData.outdoors` and skipped), and scales the plants. Per storey it lays the rugs under the pods (the rugs fixture's holders, turned with their pod), hangs the lamps where the cone pendants hang (and hides those), puts up the wall finish round the storey's windows, exit door and balcony doors (`walls.ts`, textured in meters so a brick is a brick everywhere; materials made once per interior) and the decor (`decor.ts`): ducts and I-beams (loft), pale beams and pampas grass (Skandi), stucco rosettes and frames, banker's lamps (Altbau), hanging plants and vines (jungle), a disco ball and lava lamps (70er), LED strips, neon signs on the accent wall (pictures don't hang over them) and a glow under every desk (Neon), dark beams and bonsai (Zen). Desk decor rides on the desks' groups, so it moves with the storey's pods. Everything is up out of reach, on a desk or flat on a wall: no colliders, the nav grid doesn't change.
- Own files: `src/shared/interiors.ts`, `src/server/interiors.ts`, `src/client/world/office/interior/` (`index.ts` the fixture and `Furnishing`, `paint.ts`, `walls.ts`, `lamps.ts`, `rugs.ts`, `decor.ts`), `src/client/ui/floor-interior.ts` + `.css`; `tests/interiors.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/protocol/floors.ts`: `FloorInfo.interior`. `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts` (fork file): `floor.interior`. `src/server/office/floors.ts`: `floorInfos` adds the pick. `src/server/fork/office.ts` (fork file): `interiors`. `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts` (fork file): the handler. `src/server/guests.ts`, `src/server/party.ts`.
  - Client: `src/client/world/office/build.ts` (`interior` last on the plan, `setLook` goes through it), `room.ts` (the rugs are holders, `rugs` and `pendants` handles), `ground.ts`, `balcony.ts`, `src/client/world/tower.ts` (`userData.outdoors`), `src/client/world/world.ts` (`World.setInterior`), `src/client/core/maps.ts` (paints the floor's pick with its palette), `src/client/ui/elevator.ts` (the list and the label), `src/client/ui/floormenu.ts` (the label).
  - Docs: `docs/features.md` (the floors, the tower).

### A facade for creatives

The building's outside says what goes on inside (`src/client/world/facade/`, the `facade` fixture, right after the tower on the floor's plan). The walls are anthracite now (`PALETTE.exterior`, the tower's paint), and over them, for every storey, the one you're on included:

- **Murals** across the north, east and west walls, a storey's stretch of wall one painting (`mural.ts`, canvases 56 px a meter, painted once): the agent robot with a giant pencil (IDEAS), a rocket (SHIP IT!), a light bulb (MAKE STUFF), rainbow waves round a `</>`; the storeys and walls take turns, cut round the windows, doors and the exit door. They're not merged (mergeByMaterial drops uvs).
- **Rainbow fins** down the street side between the windows, balcony doors and decks, each storey's colors one on from the one below's, with a lit front edge that makes the street side a rainbow at night.
- **A band round each storey** at its floor in its interior's accent color (shared/interiors.ts, the picks come in through `World.setBuilding` from the elevator list), its top flush with the floor, and a line of light under it in that interior's glow: from the street you see how each floor is furnished.
- **Up on the roof too.** `buildFacade(night)` is the same facade for the office (the `facade` fixture) and for the building under the roof (`world/city.ts`), the floors' picks shared between them (`setFacadePicks`); it animates itself as it's drawn. The roof's parasols stand back from the parapet for the letters.
- **Landmarks** (`landmarks.ts`): FLOGGE OFFICE in lit letters on the roof's parapet along the street side (west of the bungee jetty; from the deck you stand right at them and see them from behind, mirrored), a neon blade on the south-west corner spelling CREATE a letter at a time, a giant light bulb having an idea on an arm off the north-west corner at the top, a paper plane bobbing on a wire by the street side, a pencil as long as a car stuck point first in the east wall, and lights up the four corners and round the top going round the rainbow. Paint drips off the top of the street side (never over a window).
- The fins' places come from `src/shared/facade-fins.ts` (`finXs`, `storeyFins`), which `skin.ts` draws from and DER BRECHER's brackets keep clear of.
- Nothing has a collider; it's all outside the walls. Tests: `tests/facade.test.ts` (fins clear of every storey's openings and decks, bands under the floor, drips clear of windows).
- Hooks in upstream files: `src/client/world/office/build.ts` (`facade` on the plan), `materials.ts` (`PALETTE.exterior`), `src/client/world/tower.ts` (its paint, the parasols), `src/client/world/city.ts` (fork file: the roof's facade), `src/client/features/rooftop/world.ts` (the parasols), `src/client/world/world.ts` (`World.setBuilding`), `src/client/core/maps.ts` (sends the picks on `floors`).

### Pool party on the roof

A pool on the roof terrace for an afterwork pool party (`src/shared/roofpool.ts`, `src/client/features/roofpool/`), in its north-east corner (where the sky bar stood, clear of the elevator; the air conditioning that was screened off there is up on the elevator's housing now): a raised basin (4.8 x 5.2 m inside, `POOL`) on a wooden deck (`POOL_DECK`, 1 m up, steps up from the south, `POOL_STEPS`), tiled inside with a lane, white coping, chrome ladders, a palm in a pot on each corner of the deck, POOL PARTY 🍹 in neon over the north side, and floats drifting on the water (a flamingo, a donut, a beach ball, a unicorn; `floatAt` from the office's clock, the same on every page). The water's lit from under the surface: clear blue by day, at night going round the party colors with the lights in its walls.

- **Swimming** (`swim.ts`, `PoolSwim`): walk off the deck or jump in (**E** on the deck: a cannonball into the middle), and the pool holds you (`player.rig`) like the sea: **W A S D**, **Shift** faster, **Space** splashes, **E** at a wall climbs out onto the deck there (`climbOutAt`). Its walls keep you in; what's in your way is checked just over the roof's floor (the roof's deck is one slab, as the street is: see the swimming fix in "A day at the beach"). Nothing goes over the wire: everyone works out who's in it from their `move` (`inPoolAt`) and poses them as in the sea (`seaPose`), with a splash as they go in and rings while they swim.
- **The diving tower** (`dive.ts`, `DIVE`, `diveFloors`): legs on the deck's north side, a platform 3.5 m over the deck reaching out over the water and a yellow board on from it, rails round the rest, a ladder up its west side. **E** at the ladder (an invisible box over it catches the crosshair) climbs up (`DiveClimb`), **E** up top climbs back down; off the board you fall into the water, with a splash as big as the fall. The cannonball is only from the deck.
- **The water slide** (`slide.ts`, `SLIDE` in shared/roofpool.ts, its tube laid out from its tower's middle): a tower on the deck's south-east corner (the palm there made way), a ladder up its north face, and a tube once round it and down into the water. **E** at the ladder (or looking at the tower) climbs up and rides it (`SlideRide`, holding the player: up the rungs, then down the curve faster and faster, the first-person view looking ahead), into the pool with a big splash and swimming. Its legs and the stretch of deck it runs low over (`SLIDE_ZONE`) are in the way; climbing out never puts you there. Everyone sees riders sitting in the tube, arms up (`slidingAt`), from their `move`s; the floats keep clear of your own swimmer and of where you'll come down.
- **The sky bar moved for it** to the south end of the terrace's east side, by the bungee jetty (`ROOF_BAR` in shared/layout.ts: z -0.6 to 9.2; its pergola stops before the jetty starts). Everything about the bar follows `ROOF_BAR` (the counter, the back bar, the pergola and its lights, the SKY BAR sign, the bartender, the stools, where you order, the tower's stand-in, whereabouts).
- **On the roof** it's added the first time the roof's built (`installRoofPool`, from `parts.rooftop.roof`), its deck, steps, pots, the sign's posts and the slide pushed into the roof's colliders, interactables for the cannonball and the slide into its interactables (the water and the tower are what you look at for them: the pool's group goes into the roof's pickables) (features/rooftop/world.ts is at its size ceiling). From the floors and the street it's on the tower's roof too (`poolFromBelow`, a fixture on the office's plan).
- Tests: `tests/roofpool.test.ts` (clear of the stage, the dance floor, the elevator's doors, the bar, the fire pit, the axe lane and the dartboard, the bungee jetty, the tall tables, the table games and their players, every roof seat; steps you can walk up; in and out of the water; the floats stay on it).
- Hooks in upstream files: `src/client/main.ts` (`installRoofPool`), `src/client/world/office/build.ts` (`poolFromBelow`).

### A day at the beach

Sunset Beach on the scenic loop west of town is for playing; guests and party guests too.

- **The kiosk is open.** The snack shack by the road (it stood closed) is the *Kiosk zur Möwe*: its shutter propped up as a striped awning over the counter, a fryer, an ice cream freezer and a soft-serve machine inside, sauces and a bell on the counter, a chalkboard menu, stools, a high table and a beach flag out front. Uschi (a Person, no name tag) stands on a duckboard behind the counter and turns toward whoever comes up. **E** at the counter opens the menu (✕ top right, Esc back to mouse-look): Pommes rot-weiß, Currywurst, Fischbrötchen, Softeis, Eis am Stiel, Blue Slush, a coconut, Eistee, a Strand-Radler, all free (there's no currency in the office). She reaches over, says her line in a bubble, the bell dings (and the fryer sizzles for the hot food), and it's in your hand like the kitchen fridge's things: everyone sees it (a peer's `drink`), it comes along to every floor, food and ice cream go in bites. Some of it does something: ice cream and the slush give you brain freeze (the view shakes), the Currywurst is *mit scharf*, seagulls cry after your fries, the coconut is holiday. The Radler goes through the bar's booze and isn't served past its limit.
- **Swimming.** Walk into the sea off the sand: over the first 5 m the sand slopes away (`seaDepth`), you wade lower and slower, and then you swim (breaststroke, head up; treading water when still), **Shift** faster, **Space** splashes. Or jump off the jetty's open end or the diving board off it (a step up from the deck): you land in deep water with a splash as big as the drop. A line of red and white buoys `SWIM_OUT` (50 m) out is where the sea's fences now stand (they were 6 m out). The swim ladder down the jetty's south side at its far end, through a gap in the rail, climbs out (**E**), or swim in until you can stand (from anywhere: what stops a swimmer is checked as at the surface, since the ground under the street is one slab right across the world, the sea too, and stuck in it a swimmer could only get out toward its far edge, out to sea). It's an activity holding the player (`player.rig`), like a car: your feet go down to the depth, the head bobs at the surface. Nobody tells the office they're swimming: every page works it out from where someone is (`swimmingAt`/`wadingAt` from their `move`) and poses their body (`Person.setWorkout`), with a splash when they come in from up on the jetty and rings on the water round them.
- **Jetskis and a motorboat** at the jetty: two jetskis (one seat) and the motorboat *Möwe* (helm, the seat beside it and the stern bench: four). **E** at one takes the helm if it's free, else a free seat; **W A S D** steer, **Space** slows down, **H** sounds its horn, **E** gets off: onto the jetty's deck when you're within reach of it, else over the side into the water (inside the buoys; past them, "too far out"). They drive like the garage's cars (the cars' `drive` with their own tuning) and only on open water (`onWater`: past the shallows, not the pier, the board or the lighthouse's rocks, within the world; the sailboats and the other crafts are the driver's page to bump into). They bob, pitch up at speed and lean into turns, with spray and rings for a wake and an outboard's engine (a jetski's whine higher). Like the cars: the driver's page drives it and sends `boat.drive`, the office checks it (only the driver, only on open water, clamped to its tuning) and passes it on to the floor; `boat.enter`/`boat.leave` are answered with `boats`, a reload gets you back into your seat if it's free. Each floor has its own jetty (`Marinas`, in memory: everything's back at its mooring after a restart).

- Own files: `src/shared/beach.ts` (the sea, its depth, swimmers, the buoys, the jetty's ladder and board, `onWater`, the sailboats), `src/shared/boats.ts` (the crafts, their seats and tuning, where they're moored, `craftFits`, the messages), `src/shared/kiosk.ts` (the menu), `src/server/boats.ts` (`Marina`, `Marinas`, `boatMessage`), `src/client/features/beach/` (`index.ts` installBeach: swimming as an activity, everyone's poses and splashes; `kiosk.ts` Uschi and ordering; `ui.ts` the menu; `swim.ts` `Sea`; `poses.ts` wading, swimming, riding; `boats.ts` getting on and off, the helm activity, wakes and engines, the messages; `helm.ts` driving; `craft.ts` the models and `Flotilla`; `fx.ts` drops and rings; `sound.ts` splashes, strokes, the bell, the fryer, a gull, horns, `Outboards`), `src/client/world/scenic/kiosk.ts` (the open shack), `jetty.ts` (the board, the ladder, the sign), `buoys.ts`, `src/client/world/kioskitems.ts` (what you hold), `src/client/state/slices/boats.ts`; `tests/beach.test.ts`, `tests/boats-server.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/rooftop.ts`: `Glass`, `DrinkId` and `DRINK_BY_ID` take the kiosk's. `src/shared/fridge.ts` (fork file): `heldAnywhere`, `holdSeconds`, `isSnack` know them. `src/client/world/fridgeitems.ts` (fork file): the kiosk's shapes go to `kioskItem`.
  - `src/shared/protocol/floors.ts`: `FloorView.boats`. `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts` (fork file): `BoatClientMsg`, `BoatServerMsg`.
  - `src/server/fork/office.ts` (fork file): `marinas`. `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts` (fork file): the four `boat.*` handlers, `boatLeft` in `forkHooks` (`leaving`, `closedOn`), `boatsView`. `src/server/ws/handlers/index.ts`: `boats: boatsView` in `views`.
  - `src/server/guests.ts`: `boat.enter`, `boat.leave`, `boat.drive`, `boat.horn` in `GUEST`. `src/server/party.ts`: `boats`, `boat.move`, `boat.horn` in `PARTY_SEES`, `boats` in `VIEW_AS_IS`.
  - `src/client/world/scenic/coast.ts`: the shack is `buildKiosk`'s (and its spot handed back), the south rail stops short of the ladder (`LADDER_GAP`), `buildJettyFun` where the fence across the pier's end was, the sailboats from `SAILBOATS`. `src/client/world/scenic/water.ts`: the sea's fences out at `SWIM_OUT` (down to 2 m under the street, for swimmers), `buoys()`. `src/client/world/scenic/index.ts`: `Scenic.kiosk`.
  - `src/client/world/types.ts`: `Interactable.craft`. `src/client/sound/index.ts`: `beach()`, `setOutboards()`. `src/client/state/slices/index.ts`: the `boats` slice in `SLICES`.
  - `src/client/main.ts`: `installBeach(...)`, `__office.beach`; `src/client/core/parts.ts`: `beach`.

### The Baumarkt

HAMMER & CO, a DIY store on the landmark block north-east of the office (`landmarkBox('baumarkt')`, block (1, -2)); guests and party guests may use all of it.

- **Outside:** an orange box hall with a blue band and HAMMER & CO in huge letters, a glass shop front with sliding doors (they open for anyone near, Kalle too) under a blue canopy, a pylon sign; a car park in front with its bays, joined to the street by a driveway across the sidewalk (paved for the garage's cars, the server's check too: `onBaumarktLot` in `paved`); a trolley corral; a fenced garden centre (plants on tables, trees in pots, soil on pallets, a glasshouse roof); the delivery bay west of the hall with a curtain-sider lorry, and the dock's roller door into the hall. Everything stands clear of the street trees on the block's edge. Roofs keep the rain off (`world/roofs.ts`).
- **Inside:** four rows of high-bay racks (timber, tiles, pipes, paint, boxes on top; instanced, so the hall is a few dozen draw calls), aisle signs on wires, rows of lights, two checkouts with Gabi on the till (**E**: she rings you up for nothing), Kalle in an orange apron on his rounds by the office's clock (`kalleAt`), the paint counter with its shaker and tint turret, the tool wall, an AKTION stack. After dark the hall lights itself (`indoorGlow`: its materials glow their own colours) and the signs glow. In third person the camera stays inside the hall.
- **The forklift:** **E** on, **W S** drive, **A D** steer its rear wheels (a bicycle model about the front axle, `driveForklift`: the tail swings out), **R**/**Space** forks up, **F**/**Shift** down, **H** horn, **E** off; it beeps backing up and hums. Forks into a pallet's pockets and up: it's on (`forksIn`, `liftStep`); down to the floor where there's room: set down (`palletFits`). It stays in the hall and the bay (`FORK_AREA`), bumping off the racks, walls, counters, lorry and pallets (`forkliftFits`). Like the boats, the driver's page drives it (`bm.fork.drive`), the office checks it (only the driver, only in the area, clamped) and works out the pallets itself with the same `liftStep`, telling everyone (the driver too) when one's picked up or set down.
- **Tools and paint:** **E** at the tool wall (window, ✕/Esc) for a Bohrmaschine, Akkuschrauber, Hammer or Kettensäge; a click uses it (whirr and a spinning chuck, a ratchet, three knocks, a rasp), for everyone (`bm.use` → `bm.used`); **Q** puts it back; out of the hall the gate beeps and it goes back. **E** at the paint counter (window) picks one of ten colours: the shaker rattles the can for everyone (`bm.mix` → `bm.mixing`) and then it's in your hand (`bm.hold`, only the colour you mixed; held on the block). Held in first person in your hand's view, in third and for everyone else in the character's right fist (`Person.wear`).
- **Trolleys:** **E** to push one in front of you (`bm.trolley.*`, only on the block); let go by the corral and it's back in its slot.
- **The PA:** every 2½ minutes, at a time every page works out alike (`announcementIn`), a ding-dong-ding and an announcement as a toast, for whoever's on the block.
- Each floor has its own (`Baumaerkte`, in memory); leaving a floor or the office lets go of the forklift, the trolley and what's in your hand.

- Own files: `src/shared/baumarkt.ts` (the layout: hall, doors, bay, lorry, car park, driveway, garden centre, racks, aisles, checkouts, paint counter, tool wall, `SOLIDS`, `FORK_AREA`, `onBaumarktLot`, `rectHits`), `src/shared/baumarkt-play.ts` (the forklift, pallets, trolleys, tools, paints, the PA, `BaumarktState`, the messages), `src/server/baumarkt.ts` (`Baumarkt`, `Baumaerkte`, `baumarktMessage`), `src/client/world/baumarkt/` (`index.ts` the `baumarkt` fixture and `BaumarktWorld`; `hall.ts`, `interior.ts`, `outdoor.ts`, `models.ts`, `kit.ts`), `src/client/world/roofs.ts`, `src/client/features/baumarkt/` (`index.ts` installBaumarkt: placing what moves, the doors, the driver's seat, the camera in the hall, the PA; `forklift.ts`, `held.ts`, `trolley.ts`, `npcs.ts`, `ui.ts` + `ui.css`, `sound.ts`), `src/client/state/slices/baumarkt.ts`; `tests/baumarkt.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/garage.ts`: `paved` includes `onBaumarktLot`.
  - `src/shared/protocol/floors.ts`: `FloorView.baumarkt`. `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts` (fork file): `BaumarktClientMsg`, `BaumarktServerMsg`.
  - `src/server/fork/office.ts` (fork file): `baumaerkte`. `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts` (fork file): the `bm.*` handlers, `baumarktLeft` in `forkHooks`, `baumarktView`. `src/server/ws/handlers/index.ts`: `baumarkt: baumarktView` in `views`.
  - `src/server/guests.ts`: the ten `bm.*` messages in `GUEST`. `src/server/party.ts`: `baumarkt` and the `bm.*` server messages in `PARTY_SEES`, `baumarkt` in `VIEW_AS_IS`.
  - `src/client/world/office/build.ts`: `baumarkt` in `downstairs(...)`, after `town`. `src/client/world/sky.ts`: `sheltered` asks `underRoof` too (imported through `world/fogbox.ts`, a fork file, so sky.ts keeps its length).
  - `src/client/sound/index.ts`: `baumarkt()`, `setForklift()`. `src/client/state/slices/index.ts`: the `baumarkt` slice. `src/client/main.ts`: `installBaumarkt(...)` (its debug handle is `window.__baumarkt`).

### The cinema

The *Lichtspiele* on its landmark block behind the office (`landmarkBox('kino')`, block (-1, -1)), facing the side street at x = -28: a marquee over the entrance (now and next on its boards, live from the programme; bulbs that chase at night), a KINO blade sign, lit poster cases, a red carpet, glass doors that slide open as anyone walks up (no teleport). The foyer has the counter (Heike, a Person; **E**: popcorn, nachos, a big cola, held like the fridge's things), a popping popcorn machine, the programme board, posters. **Saal 1** shows real films back to back on the office clock; **Saal 2** plays a link someone put on at its lectern, like the office TV. Seats (**E** to sit) in both; nobody tells the office who sits where: like the beach's swimmers, every page sits a peer down who stands still on a seat's spot.

- **Films that may be shown:** the Blender open movies (Creative Commons, attribution: Big Buck Bunny, Elephants Dream, Sintel, Tears of Steel, Cosmos Laundromat (CC BY-SA 3.0 as Commons has it), Spring, Sprite Fright, Caminandes 3) and silent films public domain in Germany and the USA alike (A Trip to the Moon 1902, The Great Train Robbery 1903, The Cabinet of Dr. Caligari 1920, Nosferatu 1922: made before 1930, their directors and writers dead over 70 years; Commons' copies of them are silent, so no later score comes along). Not Chaplin, Keaton, Metropolis or Night of the Living Dead (public domain in the USA, still protected in Germany), nor Battleship Potemkin (its screenwriter died in 1974). Each streams from Wikimedia Commons' 720p VP9 transcode (`upload.wikimedia.org`, sent with CORS, so it can be a `VideoTexture`; archive.org's servers send no CORS header). Credit and licence on the poster, the screen's card, the plaque under the screen, the programme (with each film's Commons page).
- **The schedule** (`kinoAt` in `shared/kino.ts`) is a pure function of the office clock (`store.officeNow()`): from `KINO_EPOCH` the films go round, each after a 60 s break. Nothing on the server. Joining late seeks to the current position; the video is put back when it drifts 2.5 s (left alone while it's still fetching). The `<video>` is only made within 70 m of the cinema, plays only inside Saal 1 and is let go of further away. Its sound fills the hall at the TV's volume (music × master). House lights (unlit materials, `HouseLights` in `world/kino/kit.ts`), curtain and beam follow the programme.
- **Saal 2** reuses the TV: `TvStreams` (the iframe laid over its screen, `client/tv.ts`, which now takes a `ScreenLook` and keeps the canvas it lays over), `LinkPlayer` (server, `kino.json` in each floor's `.agent-office`), the TV's link rules (`parseTvUrl`), `openTvBig`. Guests and party guests may (it's play, as the TV is).
- Own files: `src/shared/kino.ts` (films, schedule, messages), `src/shared/kino-plan.ts` (walls, doors, tiers, seats, counter, as boxes over the street), `src/shared/kino-snacks.ts`, `src/server/kino.ts` (`KinoScreen`, `KinoScreens`, `kinoMessage`), `src/client/world/kino/` (`index.ts` the `kino` fixture, `shell.ts`, `marquee.ts`, `foyer.ts`, `hall.ts`, `posters.ts`, `snacks.ts`, `kit.ts`), `src/client/features/kino/` (`index.ts` installKino and the `kinocounter`/`kinobooth`/`kinoscreen` kinds, `film.ts` Saal 1's screen, `seats.ts` the `kinoseat` kind and everyone's sitting, `ui.ts` the counter, programme and lectern windows, `sound.ts`); `tests/kino.test.ts` (schedule back to back and wrapping, licence and credit for every film, stream URLs, every seat/door/the counter/the lectern reachable on foot from the street, Saal 2 for guests and party guests).
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/client/world/office/build.ts`: `kino` in `downstairs(...)`, after `town`.
  - `src/shared/rooftop.ts`: `Glass`, `DrinkId`, `DRINK_BY_ID` take the counter's. `src/shared/fridge.ts` (fork file): `heldAnywhere`, `holdSeconds`, `isSnack`. `src/client/world/fridgeitems.ts` (fork file): `kinoSnack`.
  - `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts` (fork file): `KinoClientMsg`, `KinoServerMsg`; `src/shared/protocol/floors.ts`: `FloorView.kino`.
  - `src/server/fork/office.ts` (fork file): `kinos`. `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts` (fork file): `kino.play`/`kino.stop`, `kinoView`; `src/server/ws/handlers/index.ts`: `kino: kinoView` in `views`.
  - `src/server/guests.ts`: `kino.play`, `kino.stop` in `GUEST`. `src/server/party.ts`: `kino` in `PARTY_SEES` and `VIEW_AS_IS`.
  - `src/client/tv.ts` (fork file): `ScreenLook`, the canvas kept for laying the player over. `src/client/sound/index.ts`: `kino()`.
  - `src/client/main.ts`: `installKino(...)`, `window.__kino`.
  - `src/client/ui/help.ts`, `docs/features.md`, `docs/controls.md`: words. `tests/server-dispatch.test.ts`: the welcome has `kino` before `bungee`.

### Shops to walk into

Every shop on the ground floors of the city's buildings close by (`Lot.ring` < 2, the sides that face a street) is open: walk in through its door, seamlessly, and you're in its room behind the shop front, at the same place in the city. 16 kinds (Bäckerei, Café, Pizza, Apotheke, Blumen, Buchladen, Kiosk, Bar, Späti, Friseur, Tattoo & Piercing, Döner, Spielzeug, Platten, Boutique, Optiker), dealt round in order of how far each shop's door is from the office, so the nearest 14 are one of each and every kind is all over town (`tests/shops.test.ts`). Guests and party guests may do all of it.

- **Layout, shared.** `shared/shops.ts` lays the shops out once from the city's lots (the server, the page and the tests agree): a side facing a street is split into shops of about 8 m (`lotPlan`: the ±z sides' shops run the whole width, the ±x sides' between them; as deep as half the building, at most 9 m), each with a frame of its own (`u` along the front, `v` inward), a door (`doorU`, a propped-open leaf), windows, and the kind. What's solid of a lot (`lotSolids`): with no shops all of it as before; else the floors over the shops (from `SHOP_H` up), the core behind them, and each shop's walls with the door's gap. `shared/shop-rooms.ts` furnishes each shop in its frame (`shopRoom`: the counter with the keeper behind it and where you stand, shelves, tables, chairs, crates; laid out for a door at +u and turned round otherwise, so there's a way in and across to the counter), the places where E does something (`stations`), the shop chairs as seats (`shop-<i>-<n>:0`), `lotColliders`.
- **Seen from the street.** `world/town/shops.ts` (rewritten) draws every shop's front with real windows (see-through glass, `SHOP_GLASS`), the door, awning and sign, and its room: walls, floor, ceiling and the window display, lit from inside (`litBoxMaterial` in `world/town/boxes.ts`: each vertex color glows as much as the room's lights, a little by day under the building, warm at night). `world/town/boxes.ts` piles boxes square to the world into one geometry with a color each, so all of it is a few draw calls.
- **Inside, near you.** `features/shops/` (`installShops`): the inside of the up to 6 shops within 40 m of the camera is built (one a quarter second) and let go of again past 52 m (`interior.ts`, kind by kind in `decor.ts`; merged by color, lit by the `LIT` material), with a keeper (a Person on a step behind the counter, turning toward you), the room's light on everyone in it (`setShopLights` in `world/fogbox.ts`, used by sky.ts's LIGHT), no fog in the shop you're in (`setFogShop`), the third-person camera kept in the room, and the door's bell as you come in. Kinds: `shopcounter` (the menu, `ui.ts`: everything free, the döner man's *mit alles? scharf?*), `shopchair` (the barber's and the tattoo studio's chairs: you sit, `sit` with the shop seat's key, and `openBarber` / `openInkStudio` change your look like the character window: `store.profile`, `saveProfile`, `profile`), `shopcrate` and `shoplisten` (the record shop: dig through a crate; the headphones play the record's own melody on the jukebox's synthesizer, for you alone), `shopshelf` (the bookshop's shelf). `toys.ts`: playing with a toy in your hand (click, or E with nothing in reach) and everyone's `toy.used`. `sound.ts`: the door's bell, the till, scissors, the tattoo machine, the toys, `Headphones`.
- **What you hold.** `shared/shopwares.ts`: the shops' own things (Semmel, Krapfen, pizza, the paper, gummy bears, a headache pill that sobers you up quicker, flowers, books, the bar's drinks, Döner, Dürüm, Lahmacun, Ayran, six toys, the records) and every shop's menu (`MENUS`, the fridge's, the café's and the kiosk's things too); held like the fridge's (a peer's `drink`, anywhere, along to every floor). `shared/records.ts`: a dozen records made up from a seed, `crateDig`. `world/shopitems.ts` the things in your hand, `world/sleeves.ts` the records' sleeves.
- **Looks.** `shared/avatar-marks.ts` (re-exported by `avatar.ts`): beards (`BEARD_STYLES`, the hair's color), tattoos (motif × spot, at most 6) and piercings (kind × silver/gold, at most 10) as optional fields of `Look`: absent means none, so every look saved before is the same; `sanitizeLook` checks them (unknown ids and duplicates dropped, caps), they travel in `profile` and as URL params on connecting (`marksToParams`/`marksFromParams`). Drawn by `world/character/person-marks.ts` (`person-beard.ts`, decals from `tattoo-art.ts`, small metal pieces), with a skin-colored sleeve where an arm's tattooed. `ui/barber.ts` and `ui/inkstudio.ts` (with `ui/lookpick.ts`, `ui/lookshop.css`) are the two windows; the character window has a beard row too. `tests/avatar-marks.test.ts`.
- **Boutique and optician.** Two more kinds at the end of `SHOP_KINDS`. `shared/shop-rooms.ts` (`wearRoom`): the boutique's changing cubicles along the wall away from the door (`cubicle` with its `cubiclewall`s, one or two, short of the way across to the counter; `CUBICLE_DEEP`/`CUBICLE_WIDE`), a `rack` along the door's wall and one in the middle, a `standmirror` on the back wall; the optician's `glasswall`, `eyechart`, a table of frames (`display`) and a mirror; stations `rack`, `cubicle`, `glasses`. `features/shops/wear.ts` (`wearShops`): the kinds `shoprack`, `shopcubicle`, `shopglasses` (and the counter) open `ui/wardrobe.ts` (`openBoutique`, `openOptiker`, and `outfitRows`, which the character window uses too); keeping it saves the look and the shirt color (`profile`). `features/shops/decor-wear.ts`: the rails of clothes, the cubicles with a mirror and a stool, curtains that draw shut while you or anyone on your floor stands in a cubicle (`cubicles.who`/`holds`), the wall of glasses, the eye chart; `world/town/shopwindows.ts` (`wearWindow`): mannequins and glasses on stands in the windows, from the street. The outfit is `shared/avatar-outfit.ts` (re-exported by `avatar.ts`): `top` (`TOP_STYLES`: T-shirt, hoodie, shirt, blazer, leather jacket, dress), `legs` (`LEG_COLORS`, 0 the old dark blue), `hat` (`HEADWEAR`), `specs` (`GLASSES`) and `tint` (`TINTS`, only with glasses), all optional, absent the default and never written out (`sanitizeOutfit`), compared by `sameOutfit`, on the URL as `top`/`legs`/`hat`/`specs`/`tint` (`outfitToParams`, folded into `marksToParams`/`marksFromParams`). The top's color is the profile's shirt color (`TOP_COLORS`, the character window's eight and more). Drawn by `world/character/person-outfit.ts` (`Outfit`, made by `Marks`): jacket shells and sleeves, collars, the hood, the skirt, the trousers' color, the hats, the glasses with their lenses; what pokes up of the hair hides under a hat (`hairShows`). `tests/avatar-outfit.test.ts`, and the two kinds' rooms in `tests/shops.test.ts`.
- **Server.** Held things and looks go through `act` and `profile` as they are (`heldDrink`, `sanitizeLook`). `toy.use` (only the toy in your hand, numbers clamped: `toyUse`) is passed on to the floor as `toy.used`. A shop's chair is somewhere to sit on an office floor (`server/fork/shops.ts`, `shopSeatHere`).
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/client/world/town/buildings.ts`: the lots' colliders are `lotColliders`. `src/client/world/town/index.ts`: `buildShops(group, night)` and its `light`.
  - `src/client/world/fogbox.ts` (fork file): `setFogShop`, `setShopLights`, `skyShopLight` in `FOGBOX_PARS`. `src/client/world/sky.ts`: `+ skyShopLight( vSkyWorld )` in `LIGHT` (one line).
  - `src/shared/rooftop.ts`: `Glass`, `DrinkId`, `DRINK_BY_ID` take the shops'. `src/shared/fridge.ts` (fork file): `heldAnywhere`, `holdSeconds`, `isSnack` know them. `src/client/world/fridgeitems.ts` (fork file): the shops' shapes go to `shopItem`.
  - `src/client/features/bar/booze.ts`: `letGo()` (a paper plane thrown). `src/client/sound/music.ts`: `TunePlayer`'s `seed` (a record's melody). `src/client/sound/index.ts`: `shop()`, `headphones()`.
  - `src/client/world/types.ts`: `Interactable.shop`, `.station`. `src/client/input/pointer.ts`: a click with nothing to use plays with the toy in your hand (`parts.shops.useHeld`). `src/client/features/peers/index.ts`: someone sitting in a shop's chair (`shopSeatHips`).

- **Food round 2: Eisdiele, Sushi-Bar, Metzgerei, Supermarkt** (appended to `SHOP_KINDS`). The **supermarket** is `whole`: it isn't dealt shop by shop (`DEALT_KINDS` are) but takes a whole side of two or three shops' length, every fourth such side in each quarter nearest first (`layShops`, `WHOLE_EVERY`), so one is close to the office; `QUARTER_START` is spread for the 21 dealt kinds. `shared/shop-rooms-food.ts` furnishes the four (`foodRoom`, called first in `layRoom`): the ice cream case, the sushi `beltcounter` with stools that are `belt` stations, the butcher's `meatcase`, sausages and tiles, and the supermarket's display, trolley bay (`trolleys` station), fruit stand, checkouts (the `counter` station), fridges and rows of shelves (`aisle` stations, `n` = the aisle in `MARKET_AISLES`); a station may name its own `aim` box. The belt: `beltOf`/`beltPoint`/`platePoint`/`plateAt`, plates going round on the office clock (`store.officeNow`), the same for everyone; a plate you take is hidden on your page only until it'd come round again. `shared/shopwares-food.ts`: what you hold (every 2- and 3-scoop choice of six flavours in a cone or a cup is an item of its own, `eisw…`/`eisb…`, so everyone sees your flavours; Spaghettieis, Eiskaffee, six sushi plates, green tea, Leberkässemmel, Wiener, Fleischpflanzerl, Wurstsemmel; ice has `brainfreeze`). `shared/trolley.ts`: the goods and aisles, `receipt`, the messages and `Trolleys` (who pushes one on each floor and what's in it, in memory). Client: `features/shops/food.ts` (`shopFood`: the `shopbelt`/`shoptrolleys`/`shopaisle` kinds, the parlour's picker, the checkout, E with nothing to use lets go), `trolley.ts` (the model, yours and everyone's drawn in front of whoever pushes it, let go of off the street), `decor-food.ts` (the pieces and each kind's decor, the plates on the belt), `ui-food.ts` (the flavour picker), `world/shopitems-food.ts` (held shapes), `state/slices/trolleys.ts`; `tests/shops-food.test.ts`.

Tests: `tests/shops.test.ts` counts kinds generically (≥ 14, each ≥ 4 shops in ≥ 3 quarters (5 before the bowling centre's block took its buildings' shops away; `QUARTER_START` picked again), the nearest dealt ones all different, the supermarket's checkout no menu); `tests/server-dispatch.test.ts` (welcome has `trolleys` after `kino`), `tests/client-store.test.ts` (`trolleys`).

### Shop fronts

The shops from outside, *innen wie außen*: each kind's front of its own, what's put out in front by day, and closing time on the office's clock. Guests and party guests see and use all of it (nothing goes over the wire).

- **Per kind, data-driven.** `shared/shopfronts.ts`: `FRONT_STYLES`, a table keyed by kind id (plain strings, so a kind still to come can have its row first: the Eisdiele's is waiting, tables out front), and `DEFAULT_FRONT` for any kind without one (plaster in its own frame color, a painted board, bikes, open 8 to 20). A row says the cladding (`plaster`, `wood`, `tiles`, `dark`, `colorful`, `brick`) and its color, the sign (`board`, `lightbox`, `neon`, `flicker`), the hours (`open`/`close`, past 24 is after midnight), whether a roller shutter comes down, what's put outside (`tables`, `crates`, `flowers`, `papers`, `board`, `bikes`, `gumball`) and a sandwich board's chalk. `skyHour` (the office day's hour, a day every real hour like the sky), `shopOpen`, `opensAt`, `neonLevel` (a flickering sign's stutter, from the clock alone, so everyone sees the same flicker). A new kind adds its row here; without one it still gets the default.
- **Outside, shared.** `shared/shop-outside.ts`: what stands in front of each shop (`OUTSIDE`, in the shop's frame, `v` negative), only on the strip between the sidewalk's walking band (`bandMax` + 0.15) and the front, never in front of a door or the window the passers-by look into, never on a tree or a bench; nothing that doesn't fit is put out. `outsideSolids` (a café table's are only the table and its parasol's pole, so you step up to the chairs), `OUTSIDE_SEATS` (two chairs a table, facing the street), and a `cafe` spot by the walk for each table that the passers-by sit at (one alone on a chair, two on both; `Spot.solo`, `Spot.shop`).
- **Drawn.** `world/town/shopfronts.ts` (`buildShopFronts`, from `buildTown`): per kind a few merged meshes (cladding, what's outside, the chalk, the shutters) plus one for every shop's frames, transoms, steps, shutter boxes and sign boxes, and the signs and house numbers (one atlas) merged by material: 78 meshes for the whole city. `update` each frame: shutters roll down after closing (scaled up into their box), what's outside goes in, the door's collider goes in its gap (not while you're inside, so you get out), signs: boards dim at night, lightboxes stay lit while open, neon glows with an additive halo and stutters. `world/town/shopfront-looks.ts`: the canvases (cladding per kind, the signs, the shutter's slats, the house number atlas, the chalk). The sign moved here from `world/town/shops.ts`, which exports `mergeTextured`.
- **Played.** `features/shopfronts/` (`installShopFronts`): the hour (`store.officeNow` + `store.sky.utcOffset`; `__fronts.showHour(h)` for a look from the console), `shopdoor` at a shut shop's door (*Geschlossen — öffnet um 7:00*), `cafeseat` (E sits you on a café chair, W A S D gets you up, like the cinema's seats: nobody's told, every page sits whoever's still on a chair's spot), up you get when the café shuts.
- **Passers-by.** `planFor(slot, epoch, day, hourAt?)`: with `hourAt` (`PeopleClock.hourAt`), no door and no café table of a shop that's shut at either end of the epoch; without it every shop is open, as before.
- Own files: `src/shared/shopfronts.ts`, `src/shared/shop-outside.ts`, `src/client/world/town/shopfronts.ts`, `src/client/world/town/shopfront-looks.ts`, `src/client/features/shopfronts/index.ts`, `tests/shopfronts.test.ts`.
- Hooks: `src/shared/sidewalks.ts` (`SpotKind` 'cafe', `Spot.shop`, `Spot.solo`, a door spot's shop), `src/shared/passersby.ts` (`hourAt`, `shut`, café spots), `src/client/world/town/people.ts` (`PeopleClock.hourAt`), `src/client/features/town/people.ts` (`hourAt`), `src/client/world/town/index.ts` (`fronts`), `src/client/world/town/shops.ts` (sign out, `mergeTextured` exported), `src/client/main.ts` (`installShopFronts`, `__fronts`), `src/client/ui/help.ts`, `docs/features.md`, `docs/controls.md`.

### The petrol station

FLOGGE OIL, a petrol station with a car wash, on the landmark block west of the office (`landmarkBox('tankstelle')`, x -134..-90, z -23..21): the office's street along its south side, the city's streets along its north and east. Guests and party guests may use all of it; each floor has its own, and the office keeps its clock.

- **Outside:** a canopy on four columns over two pump islands with four pumps (a display either side, hoses and nozzles both sides, the number on top), LED downlights under it (halos and a pool of light at night), a price pylon by the street (prices in the German style, *1,76⁹*), an air and water post, a vacuum station, bins, curbs, arrows, parking bays and *EINFAHRT*/*WASCHSTRASSE*/*AUSFAHRT* painted on the ground, three parked cars of its own. The driveways (in off the office's street to the pumps, in to the wash, and out of the wash across the north sidewalk onto the city's street) are paved for the garage's cars, on the page and the office alike (`paved` in `shared/garage.ts` takes `onTankstelle`). None goes further west: there the office's street is already the scenic loop, whose verge stays unbroken so a car rides along its edge (tests/scenic.test.ts, tests/driving.test.ts).
- **Filling up:** a garage car (or the Bulli) stopped beside a pump (`pumpFor`): **E** from behind the wheel (the `forecourt` activity, before the driver's in `ACTIVITY_ORDER`) or standing at the pump (`fuelpump`) sends `tank.fill`; the office checks the car's there and who's asking, picks the litres and runs the clock (7 s), every page shows the hose out of the pump into the car's filler, the display counting litres and euros, the hum; then the click and *On the house*.
- **The car wash:** stopped on the marking in the hall (`onWashBay`), **E** (or `carwash`, the terminal by the way in) sends `tank.wash`: *Bitte warten* on the signs, a red light, and the programme (`PROGRAMME`: Vorwäsche, Aktivschaum, Bürstenwäsche, Klarspülen, Trocknen, 27 s): the gantry runs along the car, its two tall brushes and the top one spin and press on it (squashing), water, three-coloured foam settling on the car and rinsed off, the dryer. A car being filled or washed stays put: the office takes no `car.drive` for it (`Forecourts.holds`) and its driver's page holds it where it stands. Then *Ausfahrt frei*, a green light, and the car's paint goes glossy with glints for ten minutes (`TankState.shine`, `features/tankstelle/gloss.ts`). Anyone standing in the hall while it sprays gets soaked (drops, a toast), never shut in.
- **The shop:** enterable through an automatic sliding door (an office `Door`), shelves, drinks fridges, the counter, the coffee machine; Jacqueline (a Person) behind the counter; **E** there (`tankshop`) opens the till (✕, Esc): Kaffee to go (the kitchen machine's buzz), Schokoriegel, Chips, Energy-Drink (a jolt of it), Bockwurst, Zeitung, held like the fridge's things.
- Own files: `src/shared/tankstelle.ts` (the layout, `STATION_PAVED`, `onTankstelle`, `inStation`, the pumps, the shop, the wash, `stationSolids`), `src/shared/tankstelle-play.ts` (fuel, `pumpFor`, `litersAt`, the programme `washAt`, `TankState`, the messages), `src/shared/tankshop.ts` (the shop's things), `src/server/tankstelle.ts` (`Forecourt`, `Forecourts`: the clock, the checks, `holds`), `src/client/world/tankstelle/` (`index.ts` the `tankstelle` fixture and `Station`, `kit.ts`, `ground.ts`, `canopy.ts` the canopy and pumps, `extras.ts` the pylon, air, vacuum, bins, `shop.ts`, `wash.ts` the hall and gantry, `washfx.ts` spray and foam, `items.ts` what you hold), `src/client/features/tankstelle/` (`index.ts` filling, the wash, the activity, the hoses, displays, wetness and sounds; `shop.ts` the cashier and till; `ui.ts` the till's window; `gloss.ts`; `sound.ts` the nozzle, the cut-off, the scanner and till, the chime, the pumps' hum and the wash's water, brushes and dryer), `src/client/state/slices/tankstelle.ts`; `tests/tankstelle.test.ts`.

### The Spielhalle and the Post (two more kinds of shop)

Two kinds appended to `SHOP_KINDS` and dealt round the city like the rest (`tests/shops.test.ts` counts kinds generically now: every kind in at least 3 quarters and 6 shops, the nearest N one of each). Guests and party guests may do all of it.

- **Spielhalle.** Neon sign and awning, a dark room with neon strips; the office's own arcade cabinet (`buildCabinet` from `features/cabinet/world.ts`) up to four times along the wall, their screens BLOCKFALL's attract mode (one canvas for all, painted a few times a second); **E** plays BLOCKFALL (`features/cabinet/blocks.ts`) in a window, for you alone (no high scores, nobody watching: that's the lounge's cabinet). A coin pusher in the middle (decor). **The claw machine**: `shared/funshops.ts` lays the pile out from the shop alone (`clawPile`, seeded: every page sees the same), `clawAim`/`clawGrab` say what a drop at (x, z) lands on and whether it holds (`GRIP` dead centre, falling off squared to nothing at a plush's edge). The window (`claw.ts`): aim with arrows/WASD, 20 s on the clock, **Space** drops; the page sends `claw.drop` and the office rolls (`Math.random`, one drop per `CLAW_EVERY` s a person) and sends `claw` (who, where, `won`) to the player and to their neighbours, so everyone near sees the claw in the shop go over, down, up and back to the chute (`motion.ts`, `clawPose`: one timeline for the window and the machine). A plush that comes down the chute is handed over like a shop's thing (`parts.shops.serve`). Decision: only the drop and its result travel, not every move of the aim (others see the claw sit still until you drop, then the whole run). **The photo booth** (`booth.ts`): curtain drawn (for you), a little WebGL renderer of its own shows your character (a `Person` with your look) on a stool; pick a pose from the emotes before each of four shots (your character in the shop plays it too, `emote` as usual); the strip of four, black and white, as a PNG to download (`<a download>`), and a `fotostreifen` in your hand.
- **Post.** Yellow sign and awning, PO boxes, parcels, a yellow letterbox, a scale on the counter. **E** at the counter writes a postcard (`post.ts`): one of six motifs of the city drawn on a canvas (`motifs.ts`, seeded, the same at both ends), up to 200 characters (`cleanPostcardText`: control, zero-width and direction-override characters out, at most 6 lines; shown as text), and who it's for: `post.recipients` answers with every account (by name) and everyone in now, never yourself; party guests get only whoever's in (`postRecipients`). `post.send` is checked against that list, `POSTCARDS_PER_DAY` (10) a sender a day; kept in `postcards.json` in the data folder (`server/postcards.ts`, at most 2000 waiting) until the recipient is in: at once when they're online (`post.cards`: a toast, and the card in a window), else when their page asks (`post.check`, once it's in, or **E** at the PO boxes).
- Own files: `src/shared/funshops.ts`, `src/shared/plushies.ts`, `src/server/postcards.ts`, `src/server/ws/handlers/funshops.ts`, `src/client/features/funshops/` (`index.ts` installFunShops: the interaction kinds `shopcabinet`, `shopclaw`, `shopbooth`, `shoppobox`, the messages; `decor.ts`, `motion.ts`, `claw.ts`, `booth.ts`, `blockfall.ts`, `post.ts`, `motifs.ts`, `ui.css`); `tests/funshops.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream and fork files:
  - `src/shared/shops.ts`: the two kinds at the end of `SHOP_KINDS`. `src/shared/shop-rooms.ts`: their pieces and stations (`cabinet`, `claw`, `booth`, `pobox`). `src/shared/shopwares.ts`: the plushies and the strip as shop items, glasses `plush`/`photostrip`, `MENUS.spielhalle` (snacks), `MENUS.post` (none: the counter writes cards). `src/client/world/shopitems.ts`: their shapes in your hand.
  - `src/client/features/shops/index.ts`: `KIND_OF` names the new stations' kinds, `ShopsDeps.special` (the Post's counter), `keeper(i)` returned. `decor.ts`: its helpers exported, `default:` hands over to `funDecor`. `interior.ts`: the neon kinds' lamps and counter by `k.neon`.
  - `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts`: `ClawClientMsg`, `ClawServerMsg`, `PostClientMsg`, `PostServerMsg`. `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts`: `...funshopHandlers`. `src/server/fork/office.ts`: `postcards`, `owner` exported. `src/server/guests.ts`: `claw.drop`, `post.*` in `GUEST`. `src/server/party.ts`: `claw`, `post.*` in `PARTY_SEES`.
  - `src/client/main.ts`: `installFunShops(...)`, `special` for `installShops`; `src/client/core/parts.ts`: `funshops`. `src/client/ui/help.ts`, `docs/features.md`, `docs/controls.md`: words.

### Bikes, pets and laundry (three more kinds of shop)

`fahrrad`, `zoo` and `waschsalon` appended to `SHOP_KINDS`; `QUARTER_START` in `shared/shops.ts` re-picked so all 21 kinds get at least six shops in three quarters (with more kinds, pick again: the comment says how). Guests and party guests may do all of it.

- **Fahrradladen.** Bikes on the wall and in floor racks, the repair stand (its back wheel spinning), tyres, bikes parked out front on the sidewalk and one in the window (`features/shops/decor-ride.ts`, the bike itself `world/bike.ts`: sized for the chibi people, wheels and cranks turning with `roll(meters, steer)`, `ridingPose` for `Person.setWorkout`). **E** at the counter (`ShopsDeps.rideCounter`) opens the rental (`features/ride/ui.ts`): Hollandrad, Rennrad, BMX, Lastenrad (`shared/ride.ts` `BIKES`: the garage's `drive` model with their own tuning, all quicker than running and slower than the cars; `hop`). You're put on it outside the door. `features/ride/rider.ts` takes hold of you like the beach's Helm (`PlayerController.rig`, `riding`): W/S/A/D, Space hops, kerbs ridden up, what you'd bump into stops you, and `rideable` keeps bikes out of every shop's room. **H** rings the bell (`bike.bell`, passed on to your floor), **E** gets off: the bike waits where you left it (drawn for you only; `parkedbike` to get back on). Back in a bike shop on foot, or after `RENTAL_SECONDS`, it's given back. **Sync:** no vehicle state of its own: your page moves you with the usual `move`, and `bike.ride` sets `PeerInfo.bike` on the server (`server/fork/ride.ts`), broadcast as `bike.rode`, so everyone (and anyone arriving later, through the welcome's peers) sees you on the bike: `features/ride/riders.ts` puts a bike under each rider's `Person` and pedals their legs as far as they've come.
- **Zoohandlung.** Aquariums (water, gravel, weed, fish swimming the tank's length, bubbles), budgies in hanging cages and on a stand, a hamster in its wheel, food on shelves. Its menu: `goldfisch` (a knotted bag of water, `world/shopitems.ts` `fishbag`, sloshing by itself in `onBeforeRender`) and `wellensittich`: both held like any shop thing (`PeerInfo.drink`, so synced, along to every floor, gone after `seconds`). What's in `ON_SHOULDER` isn't drawn in the hand but sits on the right shoulder of whoever holds it (`riders.ts`, on `Person.bones.body`, bobbing and looking about), so no new look field was needed.
- **Waschsalon.** Washing machines along a wall (`shop-rooms-ride.ts`: one `washer` station each, with its own `hit` box over its machine), dryers stacked two high, the folding table, plastic chairs along the window (shop chairs: `sit` as usual, `sitIn` just sits you there), the vending machine (`shopvending`: the shop menu with the fridge's snacks). Drums tumble laundry and spin while running: yours (`startWash`, local to your page, with the coin, the rumble and a ding at the end) or somebody's (`busyMachine`, from the wall clock: the same for everyone, nothing sent).
- Own files: `src/shared/ride.ts`, `src/shared/shop-rooms-ride.ts`, `src/server/fork/ride.ts`, `src/client/world/bike.ts`, `src/client/features/shops/decor-ride.ts`, `src/client/features/ride/` (`index.ts` installRide: the rental, the `cyclist` activity, the parked bike; `rider.ts`, `riders.ts`, `laundry.ts`, `ui.ts`, `sound.ts`); `tests/ride.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream and fork files:
  - `src/shared/shops.ts`: the three kinds, `QUARTER_START`. `src/shared/shop-rooms.ts`: `RidePieceKind`, `RideStationKind`, `Station.hit`, `default: layRide(...)`, no window display in the Waschsalon, `turned` mirrors `hit`. `src/shared/shopwares.ts`: the four items, their glasses, `PetItemId`, the `pet` treat, `MENUS`.
  - `src/client/features/shops/index.ts`: `KIND_OF` (`washer`, `vending`), `ShopsDeps.rideCounter`, the Waschsalon's chairs in `sitIn` and the chair's hint; `refresh` makes room for a nearer shop when six are open (`>= MAX`), so a shop's inside is built as you walk in. `interior.ts`: `default: furnishRide(...)`, a station's `hit`. `decor.ts`: `default:` also `rideDecor(...)`. `src/client/world/shopitems.ts`: `fishbag`, `budgie`, `detergent`, `sock`.
  - `src/shared/protocol/presence.ts`: `PeerInfo.bike`. `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts`: `RideClientMsg`, `RideServerMsg`. `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts`: `bike.ride`, `bike.bell`. `src/server/guests.ts`, `src/server/party.ts`: the same, sorted.

### Traffic lights and the city bus

The busier crossings round the office (three or four ways, within 175 m) have traffic lights, and the Flogge Verkehrsbetriebe's buses run fixed lines through the streets, all on the office's clock, so everyone sees the same thing with nothing on the wire but where someone stands in a bus. At each road coming in: a pole on its right before the stop line with red, amber and green (red and amber together the second before green), a white stop line, and at both ends of its zebra the red and the green man; the lamps glow at night. The two roads take turns (40 s: 15 green, 3 amber, 2 red both ways, each), offset crossing to crossing. The city's cars (still each page's own) stop at the line on red and amber (going on over amber only when they couldn't stop and are over before the red), stop behind a bus, and turn round out of the way of one coming up behind them. The passers-by wait at the curb for the green man where there are lights (and only set off while there's time to get across). A garage car over a red line at more than 30 km/h gets flashed (your page only).

**The network (Florian: "aktive Buslinien, feste Linien, Busse ausstatten, begehbar zur Mitfahrt").** Three lines, each a loop past the office's front with two buses: the **7** *Ring* (Lindenallee, Bowlingcenter, Flogge Office, Kino, Baumarkt), the **12** *Stadtpark* (Nordstadt, Stadtpark, Kirche, Kino, Soccerhalle, Bowlingcenter, Baumarkt) and the **3** *Südstadt* (Marktplatz, Südstadt, Casino, Soccerhalle, Gym · Schallwerk, Kino, Tankstelle). Every stop has a name and a place of its own (`STOP_DEFS`: street, side, where along it; a shelter on the strip, or only the pole at the curb where there's no strip: the office's street, in front of a landmark), on the side the line drives on; the street furniture makes room for them, and the random stops of before are benches now. A line's first stop is its terminus, where a bus that's early waits for its next departure.

**The timetable.** Worked out once on every page (and the office): every bus in town is driven together, 0.05 s steps, from two periods back, against the lights' clock (`mayPass`), the corners and the stops, and each stops short of another bus in its way where that one is now (outline against outline, 26 m ahead: the one in front at a stop or a light, one turning across it; of two in each other's way the earlier in `BUS_RUNS` goes first). Each line's round fits a whole number of times into `BUS_PERIOD` (26 light cycles, 1040 s: the 7 and the 12 three rounds, the 3 two), so after the warm-up the town settles into a period that repeats for ever; the last period is kept per bus (`BusRun.track` every 0.25 s, `calls`). `poseOf`/`busAt` read it, `busCall` gives the passers-by the next call at their stop (with time enough to get on or off), `nextCalls` the boards their next buses, `nextStop` the displays theirs. The tests check no two buses' outlines ever overlap, every bus calls at every stop every round with its doors open, crosses stop lines only on green or amber, and comes round seamlessly.

**The buses.** Low-floor city buses in their line's color: the line and destination in amber dots over the windscreen and on the side, "Flogge Verkehrsbetriebe" along both sides, two double doors on the right that slide out and apart at a stop. Inside (`shared/buscabin.ts`, one plan for drawing, walking and the office): seats down both sides (two a row on the left, one on the right, the back row across; the ones by the middle doors red), the pram space across from the middle doors, yellow poles with red stop buttons, rails and straps under the ceiling, a validator by each door, the driver (a `Person`, hands on the wheel) in the cab behind a screen, two displays over the aisle with the next stop, the line and the time (*HALT* once you've pressed the button), the line's stops along the left wall with the next one lit, two of the town's ads over the right-hand windows. Merged into a few meshes per bus; the displays redraw only when what they say changes, and only within 70 m (the drivers too). Every stop's pole carries the H, its name with the lines that call there, and a departures board (`world/town/busstops.ts`), drawn when you're within 35 m and only when a minute changes.

**Riding.** E at an open door gets you on just inside it. Your page keeps you in the bus's own frame (`features/citybus/cabin.ts`: x along it, z across, the doors at +z) and puts you in the world from it every frame, so the bus carries you round corners and all: walk about with W A S D (2.3 m/s, round the seats, the cab and the validators), E sits you in the nearest free seat (hips 0.5, the view with it) or gets you up, stamps a ticket at the validator, or gets you off at an open door; walking out of an open door gets you off too; H presses the stop button. E picks whichever is nearest. The player's rig has a floor (`PlayerController.rigFloor`), so you're on your feet, not falling. Your page tells the office where in which bus you are (`bus.ride`, at most every 120 ms, only when it changes); the office keeps it as `PeerInfo.bus` (only a bus that's there, a place that's in it, a seat that's one; `busRideOf`) and passes it to your floor (`bus.rode`), and every page draws you right there in the bus as it is this frame (`aboardBus`, from `features/peers`), standing, walking or sitting, not a step behind it as following your `move`s would; the people list says "🚌 riding on the 7 to Ring". Leaving the floor or a reconnect gets you off. As it pulls away the gong and the browser's German voice call the next stop ("Nächster Halt: Kino", at the office sounds' volume), as it comes in its name, at the terminus "Endhaltestelle". Engine, door hiss, gong, stop button and validator are synthesized. Guests and party guests ride too.

**On the maps.** The big map (J) draws every line's route in its color, the stops (named zoomed in) and every bus as a tag with its number where it is now; the minimap the buses round about and, zoomed in, the stops (`features/minimap/buses.ts`).

- Own files:
  - `src/shared/traffic-lights.ts`: `LIT` (which crossings), `lampAt`, `walkAt`, `walkStart`, `mayPass`, `nextLight`, `lightLimit` (how far a car's front may go).
  - `src/shared/busnet.ts`: `STOP_DEFS` (the named stops), `LINE_DEFS` (the lines: round, stops, offset, buses, rounds), `POLE_OFF`.
  - `src/shared/citybus.ts`: `BUS_LINES` (the loops, their stops and stop lines), `BUS_RUNS` (every bus and its timetable, from driving the town), `BUS_PERIOD`, `poseOf`/`busAt`, `busCall`, `nextCalls`, `nextStop`.
  - `src/shared/buscabin.ts` (the inside: `SEATS`, `VALIDATORS`, `CAB`, `CABIN_BLOCKS`, `standsAt`, `doorAt`, `inDoor`, `inCabin`), `src/shared/busride.ts` (`BusRide`, the `bus.ride`/`bus.rode` messages, `busRideOf`, `busWhereabouts`).
  - `src/server/fork/busride.ts` (`busRideMessage`, `busLeft`).
