### The phone

Florian: "das Handy per se gehört dazu". **I** takes your phone out: a phone held up in the middle of the screen (a modal: ✕, Esc or I again puts it away and gives you the mouse back), with the time, "Flogge 5G" and its home screen of apps. Everyone on your floor sees it in your hand, arm out in front of you (`PeerInfo.phone`, `phone.hold` → `phone.held`; the office takes it at most every 150 ms; guests and party guests too). Apps are added by features through a registry (`addPhoneApp`: id, name, icon, color, an optional badge, `open(screen, phone)` drawing into the screen and handing back its cleanup), shown in the order they're added: **Waymo** (features/waymo/app.ts), **Bus** (features/citybus/app.ts: the four stops nearest you with their next departures counting down, tap one and the minimap points you there), **Karte** (features/minimap: opens the big map). The minimap points you anywhere another feature says (`headTo`, features/minimap/goal.ts).

- Own files: `src/shared/phone.ts`, `src/server/fork/phone.ts` (`phoneMessage`), `src/client/features/phone/index.ts` (`installPhone`: the phone, I, `window.__phone` with `open(app?)`, `close`, `isOpen`), `apps.ts` (`addPhoneApp`, `phoneApps`), `prop.ts` (`holdPhone`: the phone in a `Person`'s hand), `ui.css` (the phone and its apps' lists, the Waymo app's too), `src/client/features/citybus/app.ts`, `src/client/features/minimap/goal.ts` (`headTo`, `headingTo`, `bindGoal`).
- Hooks in upstream files: `src/client/main.ts` (`installPhone`), `src/shared/protocol/presence.ts` (`PeerInfo.phone`), `src/client/features/peers/index.ts` (`holdPhone` for everyone else), `src/server/guests.ts`, `src/server/party.ts`, `src/client/ui/help.ts`, `docs/controls.md`, `docs/features.md`.

### Waymo

Florian (who rode one in 2024: "das ist crazy, das muss ins Spiel"): Waymo robotaxis, called on the phone, driving themselves, friends riding along, the screen of what the car sees, the wheel turning by itself, an empty fleet cruising about.

**The streets as they drive them** (`shared/waymo/roads.ts`): the crossings are nodes, the stretches between them lanes in either direction (`LANES`, from `CROSSINGS`' ways). `route` finds the crossings to drive through from one place on a lane to another (Dijkstra over lanes, turns cost a little, never round on the spot, the lanes the buses drive cost 2.5× so they're used only when there's no good way round, `avoid` for lanes it mayn't take), `pathOf` lays that as a lane line every half meter as the buses' are (right-hand lane, curves at the corners, `gates` at the lit crossings' stop lines, `corners`, `legs` for where on which lane each straight bit is). A robotaxi waits (to pick up, to let out) only at `KERB_SPOTS`: every 6 m along the lanes no bus drives (`BUS_LANES`, from the bus network), 16 m clear of the crossings, so nothing ever stands in a bus's way; `kerbNear` finds the one nearest a place (by the office: round its east side, 28 m from the door).

**Drives** (`shared/waymo/drive.ts`): the office drives each robotaxi's path ahead of time in 0.05 s steps, keeping where it is every 0.25 s: up to 40 km/h, slowing for the corners, stopping at red (`mayPass`, as the buses), and stopping short of anything in its way where that'll be at the time (the buses off their timetable, the other robotaxis off their drives; outline against outline, 30 m ahead). The others don't wait for it, so if one would still run into it (a bus from behind while it waits at a red light), the drive fails and the office tries again a few seconds later, then another way round the lane where it went wrong. Each drive goes over the wire as its ends, its crossings, when it set off and its quarter-second samples; every page lays the same path (`pathFor`) and plays it back off the office's clock (`waymoAt`). About 40 ms of the office's time a drive.

**The fleet** (`server/fork/waymo.ts`, `shared/waymo/fleet.ts`): eight cars, one fleet for the building (the street's the same under every floor), spread round town at the start. With nothing to do one cruises to a curb at least 150 m off, and on. **Booking** (`waymo.book`: where you are, where to): the four free cars nearest your curb are each given a way there, and the one that gets there first comes (`coming`; a car mid-drive branches off where it's next on a straight bit of lane, finishing its old drive up to there, `prev`), your initials on its dome; at the curb it waits three minutes (`waiting`). **E** at it (`waymo.enter`) puts you in the first free of its four seats (beside the empty driver's, three at the back); your friends can get into any waiting car. **Fahrt starten** (`waymo.go`, any rider): a drive from the curb to the curb nearest where you said (`riding`), doors locked; **Rechts ran** (`waymo.pullover`): a new drive to the curb nearest where it'll be in six seconds. There (`arrived`), it waits for everyone to get out (`waymo.leave`, up to 90 s) and cruises on. Cancel (`waymo.cancel`) while it comes or waits; leaving your floor or the office gets you out and your booking off. Honk (`waymo.honk`, the booker): everyone near hears it and sees its lights flash. Every change goes to the whole building (`waymo.car`); a page that's come asks for all of them (`waymo.hello` → `waymo.fleet`). Guests and party guests ride too.

**On your page** (`features/waymo/`): the cars (`world/waymo/model.ts`: the I-Pace's shape in white over black cladding, the dark glass cabin, "waymo" on the front doors, sensor pods on the corners and the mirrors, the lidar dome spinning on the roof with four little displays round its base showing the rider's initials in teal, the seats, the dash, a screen on it and on the back of each front seat, the wheel turning by itself, the wheels rolling, the indicators blinking 25 m before a corner, the brake lights) where the office's clock has them, each with a collider. The city's cars stop for them (`features/town`: three points nose to tail, as for the buses). In the car: your page keeps you in your seat as it goes (hips 0.32 over the street, the view 0.2 m lower than standing, turning with the car; hands hidden, `rigFloor`), E and Q as above, the hint says how long till you're there; everyone else sees you sitting in it this very frame (`aboardWaymo` from features/peers; "🚕 in a Waymo to Kino" in the people list). A gong when it sets off, a ding when it's there, toasts for the car coming, waiting and there; the minimap points you to the curb (`headTo`). The ride's screens (`screen.ts`, about 9 frames a second, only in your car): the streets round about as dark asphalt, the lidar's rings, the route ahead in blue with its end, the city's cars (purple), the buses (amber), the other robotaxis (white) and the people (orange dots) as the lidar sees them, where it's going, the ETA and the speed; waiting, "Fahrt starten (E)". The maps draw every robotaxi as a little white car, yours in teal with your initials (`features/minimap/waymos.ts`). `window.__waymo` has `riding` and `fleet` for a look from the console.

- Own files: `src/shared/waymo/roads.ts`, `drive.ts`, `fleet.ts`; `src/server/fork/waymo.ts` (`Fleet`), `src/server/ws/handlers/waymo.ts` (`waymoHandlers`, `waymoHooks`); `src/client/world/waymo/model.ts` (`buildWaymo`, `DomeSign`); `src/client/features/waymo/index.ts` (`installWaymo`), `state.ts` (`fleet`, `posesNow`, `seatOf`, `aboardWaymo`, `waymoWhereabouts`), `screen.ts` (`RideScreen`, `idleScreen`), `app.ts` (`waymoApp`); `src/client/features/minimap/waymos.ts` (`drawWaymos`); `tests/waymo.test.ts` (the streets and routes on the road, curbs off the bus lanes, 10 minutes of the fleet cruising without running into a bus or each other and only over stop lines on green or amber, a ride from booking to getting out, cancelling, waiting too long, leaving).
- Hooks in upstream files: `src/client/main.ts` (`installWaymo`), `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts` (`WaymoClientMsg`, `WaymoServerMsg`), `src/server/fork/office.ts` (`waymo: Fleet`, started and stopped with the fork), `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts` (the handlers, `waymoHooks` on leaving and closing), `src/server/guests.ts` (`WAYMO_CLIENT_MSGS`), `src/server/party.ts` (`WAYMO_SERVER_MSGS`), `src/client/features/peers/index.ts` (`aboardWaymo`), `src/client/ui/whereabouts.ts` (`waymoWhereabouts`), `src/client/ui/help.ts`, `docs/features.md`, `docs/controls.md`.
- In fork files: `src/client/features/town/index.ts` (the city's cars stop for the robotaxis), `src/client/features/minimap/index.ts` and `bigmap.ts` (the robotaxis on the maps, `bindGoal`, the map app).

### Sounds of the city

The city round the office (*One city round the office*) can be heard: the road's rumble (brown noise and a tyre hiss), as loud as how near the nearest street is (counting how high up you are) and how many of the city's cars are about, faint from the floors and the roof, quieter and muffled (a 650 Hz low-pass) behind glass, nothing 90 m from any street; a whoosh with a falling band as a city car passes within 9 m; birds in the parks' and streets' trees within 40 m by day, crickets under them on warm dry nights (`summerish`: over 14 °C by the forecast, else May to September), neither in the rain nor behind glass (the office's windows keep their own); the murmur of people by the shops (three formant voices swelling and fading, `density` from the passers-by), a café's cups clinking; the rain on the shops' awnings, a patter and drops. A church with a bell tower on a park 80–200 m from the office (the nearest with a clear patch of grass: (-45, -110), on block (-1, -2), the tower toward the office) rings at 8, 12 and 18 of the office's sky (`BELL_HOURS`: a day goes by in an hour, so every hour would be every 2.5 minutes), the hour's count on a twelve-hour clock; a siren (a two-tone Martinshorn) goes by 220–380 m off every 10–20 minutes of the office's clock (`siren(n)`: n × 15 min ± 2.5 min, hashed, its bearing and way round too), rising over eight seconds and fading. Bells and sirens are on `store.officeNow`, the same moment for everyone, and never late: a jump of more than 10 s (a hidden tab, the page opening) rings nothing. Everything goes out on one bus of its own into the ambience bus, so it follows the office sounds' volume and mute and goes quiet when the tab is hidden; the **Stadtgeräusche** setting (on by default) fades it out. No wire traffic. `window.__citySound()` gives the levels, the loops' gains, the muffle's cut-off and the counts of what's played, for checks from the console.

  - `src/shared/church.ts`: `CHURCH` (which park, where on it, which way, the tower, the bells, the nave's and tower's boxes), laid out clear of the park's and the streets' trees.
  - `src/client/world/church/index.ts`: the church (nave, gable roof, windows, tower with louvres, spire, cross), the `church` fixture in the outlook, with its colliders.
  - `src/client/sound/city.ts` (`CitySound`: the loops, the whoosh, birds, crickets, cups, awning drops; `probe()`), `src/client/sound/citybells.ts` (`bells`, `siren`).
  - `src/client/features/citysound/index.ts`: `installCitySound`: each frame (`env`) where the street is from where you are (on the roof `-roofDrop`), the city cars' middles (read from `office.town.traffic`, `world/town/traffic.ts` is untouched), in a shop or not, warm or not; the bells and sirens on the office clock; `window.__citySound`.

### The minimap

With the town round the office (*One city round the office*) and the loop past the beach, a way to find your way back. A round map in the bottom-right corner (top right on a phone) turns with the camera, straight ahead up, with an **N** on its rim: drawn once onto a canvas (2 px a meter, 660 × 810 m) from the same plans the town, the loop and the coast are built from (`BLOCKS`, `LOTS`, `PARK_TREES`, `STREETS`, `PAVEMENT`, `LOOP`, `shoreX`, `FARM`, `LAKE`, `CREEK`, `MOUNTAINS`, the landmarks' boxes, the buildings across the street, `SHOPS` in their awnings' colors), so it never disagrees with the world. Each frame (`render`, after the camera) it cuts the bit round you out of that, then draws upright on top: the places and (zoomed in past 1.2 px/m) the shops by their emoji, the people on your floor (`store.peers`, `onMyFloor`, not on /lite) as dots, you as an arrow, and the office (`HOME`: the foot of the steps up to the exit door) and the picked place as badges, or at the rim as arrows when they're out of sight. Under it, where you are in words (the office, a shop by `shopAt`, a place within 16 m, a bit of the loop by `placeAt`, Downtown) and the way to the office or the picked place with distance and compass word; the pick is crossed off within 8 m. In a place of its own (the casino, gym, padel and soccer halls, whose rooms have their own numbers) you're shown at its building; off the office's own map (the castle) it hides. The wheel zooms it (0.5–4 px/m, kept in `localStorage`). **J** or a click opens the big map (a modal, north up, drag and wheel, `+`/`−`, shop names past 2.4 px/m, name tags that don't overlap, the office's and the pick's first): click a place to pick it, **Zum Büro**, **Ziel löschen**, **🎯 Ich**. Only on your page: nothing on the wire. **☰ → Minimap** hides it (a HUD panel, on by default); while it shows, the side panels stop short of it.

  - `tests/minimap.test.ts`.

  - `src/client/state/persist.ts`: `HudPanel` `map`, on in `HUD_DEFAULTS`. `src/client/ui/menu.ts`: its row in `PANELS` and `PANEL_EL`.
  - `src/client/main.ts`: `installMinimap(ctx)`. `src/client/ui/help.ts`: two rows. `docs/features.md`, `docs/controls.md`.

### Hörkreise

Voice carries as far as each speaker's own circle (the Hörkreis), not over the whole floor fading out but never silent, as upstream has it. Whoever stands in your circle hears you, louder the closer (full up to half the circle, at least 3 m, 0.45 at the edge, out over 1 m past it); outside it nobody does, because your mic isn't sent to them at all (`Voice.setSending`: the mic's sender for that peer carries no track, the connection stays up for when they come close). So two people with small circles can talk unter vier Augen while everyone stays in the same voice chat. **,** makes yours smaller, **.** bigger (×1.25 a step, 1.5–60 m, kept in `localStorage`); the top bar's **◯ 15 m · 👂 2** chip while you're in voice says how big it is and how many hear you (its title names them) and a click goes through Flüstern 2 m, Gespräch 5 m, Raum 15 m (everyone's start), Rufen 30 m, Megafon 60 m. Your circle shows on the floor in your color while you talk and for a moment after you change it; anyone else's while they talk, bright when you're in it (you hear them), faint when you're not. Who gets your voice is worked out every 200 ms on an interval (so it keeps up with the tab in the background), with 1.5 m of slack past the edge once you're sending so walking along it doesn't cut in and out; a new connection starts without your voice until then. On the karaoke stage with a mic (`Voice.selfOnPa`) the whole bowling centre gets you, whatever your circle. Someone on another floor or on /lite never does.

- Own files: `src/shared/voicerange.ts` (the sizes, `heardVolume`, `sendsVoice`, `voiceRangeWord`, the messages `voice.range` / `voice.ranged`), `src/server/fork/voicerange.ts` (`voiceRangeMessage`: kept as the peer's `voiceRange`, told to everyone), `src/client/features/voicerange/` (`index.ts`: keys, who gets your voice, the circles; `state.ts`: your circle; `hud.ts`: the top bar's button; `rings.ts`: a circle on the floor), `tests/voicerange.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/protocol/presence.ts`: `PeerInfo.voiceRange`. `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts` (fork file): the messages. `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts`, `src/server/guests.ts` (`GUEST`), `src/server/party.ts` (`PARTY_SEES`).
  - `src/client/voice.ts`: `Conn.unheard`, `setSending`, `sendingTo`, `selfOnPa`, and new connections and joining voice start without sending until the circle says.
  - `src/client/features/peers/index.ts`: each voice's volume is `heardVolume(d, peer.voiceRange)`.
  - `src/client/features/hud/index.ts`: `voiceRangeAction(voice)` after the mute button. `src/client/main.ts`: `installVoiceRange(...)`. `src/client/features/karaoke/index.ts` (fork file): `selfOnPa`.
  - `src/client/ui/help.ts`, `README.md`, `docs/features.md`, `docs/controls.md`.

### Dancing on the roof

Florian's wish: real dance moves on the roof's dance floor, as long as you like and on the beat, not an emote that's over in four seconds. Up on the roof **B** starts dancing (the move you danced last, Freestyle the first time); **1–0** pick one of the first ten moves, **Q** / **E** go through all thirteen, **F** is Freestyle, a click on the row of moves over the hint bar works too, and **B** again, walking off, sitting down, anything else you start or going downstairs stops it (walking off with a toast: *B tanzt weiter*). The moves: Two-Step, Running Man, Robot, Shuffle, Disco-Fieber, Arm Wave, Floss, Hands Up (jumpstyle), Headbang, Vogue, Moonwalk, Hip-Hop Bounce (Dougie) and Macarena. Freestyle picks them by itself as the music goes: a new one every two or four bars (by who's dancing, so not everyone changes at once, never the same twice running), the wilder ones in a drop, swaying with hands up waving in a breakdown, crouching lower and pumping through a build, clapping overhead at its top, a jump and a spin as the drop lands. Every move is a function of the set's beat count (whole counts land on a beat; drum and bass at 172 is danced half-time), which every page works out from the office's clock or the set's own beats, so whoever watches sees you hit the same beat as you do, and Freestyle's choices go by the music and your id, the same everywhere. Snappy hits on the beat, smooth in between, blended into each new move (exp smoothing, worked out 50 ms ahead so the easing doesn't lag the beat); the knees bend by spreading the legs and the hips come down so a foot stays on the floor; how hard the music goes scales the bounce and the hops; reduced motion tones it all down (no hops or spins). On the dance floor a soft spot pulses under each dancer in the track's colour, and a "B · Tanzen" pill shows while you're on it. In first person the dance comes to you (`features/dance/firstperson.ts`): the view rides your hips and head (the bounce and hops, the sway, a tilt of the shoulders, the nod, a Headbang's bang; no spins), and your hands do the move's arms in front of your eyes (up and out of sight for Hands Up, out to the sides for the Arm Wave, framing your face for Vogue), read off the same eased shape your body is in (`Dancer.shape`, `bodyY`), through `Hands.dancing` and a `ctx.view` effect. The line under your name says what you dance. Guests and party guests dance too.

- Own files: `src/shared/dance.ts` (the moves, `isDance`, `danceCounts`/`danceTempo` (half-time), Freestyle's `freestyleAt`/`danceSeed`, `danceWhereabouts`, the messages `dance.set` / `dance.moved`), `src/server/fork/dance.ts` (`danceMessage`: only on the roof, a valid move, throttled, kept as the peer's `dance`, told to everyone; `danceLeft` on going downstairs), `src/client/features/dance/` (`index.ts`: the keys, the activity and its hint, telling the office, posing you and everyone else with `Person.setWorkout` (only ever taking back our own pose), the picker's state; `poses.ts`: the moves as shapes of the body by beat count, and `Dancer`, which eases into them and keeps a foot on the floor; `ui.ts` + `ui.css`: the picker and the pill; `glow.ts`: the spots on the dance floor), `tests/dance.test.ts`.
- Hooks in upstream files:
  - `src/shared/protocol/presence.ts`: `PeerInfo.dance`. `src/shared/protocol/fork.ts` (fork file): the messages. `src/server/ws/handlers/fork.ts` (fork file): `dance.set`, and `danceLeft` in `forkHooks.leaving` off the roof. `src/server/guests.ts` (`GUEST`), `src/server/party.ts` (`PARTY_SEES`).
  - `src/client/features/rooftop/index.ts`: `frame()`, what the roof moves to (the set that's on or the house DJ), returned for the dance. `src/client/main.ts`: `installDance(...)`, `__dance`.
  - `src/client/ui/whereabouts.ts`: `danceWhereabouts` on the roof. `src/client/ui/help.ts` (a row), `README.md`, `docs/features.md`, `docs/controls.md`.

### The bowling centre

On the block right next to the office to the east (block (1, 0), `landmarkBox('bowling')`, the house in `BOWLING_BOX` x 35..77, z -22..20, its front on the office's street), a bowling centre: six lanes with a league, a counter, cosmic bowling, a karaoke bar and a black-light mini golf room. The plan all four halves build on is `src/shared/bowling.ts` (`BOWLING = '@bowling'`, the box, the room, `ZONES`: who builds where, the lanes' numbers, `BowlingLights`), and the seam they join by is `src/client/world/bowling/parts.ts` (`addBowlingPart({ build, use, hint, onMessage, update, place, lights })`). This section is the building's half; the lanes, the karaoke bar and the mini golf have their own subsections below.

**The building.** A 50s American bowling centre, cream render with a teal plinth, a teal band and cherry pinstripes all round, a teal coping. The middle of the front is a curved glass bay (an arc through both ends of a 15 m opening, its ends 2.6 m back) with chrome mullions and the lobby lit behind it (a painted view of the counter, the lanes through an opening, the arcade), sliding glass doors in its apex that open for anyone near (`site.doors`), a rubber mat, under a Googie boomerang canopy with a pink neon edge and downlights (their halos on the sidewalk at night). Over it BOWLING CENTER in a teal script between two starbursts, more starbursts across the front (they glow at night), lit posters either side (Cosmic Bowling, Karaoke, Schwarzlicht-Minigolf, what the counter has today), palms in teal planters, a cherry bench and a bin, a rack of chrome hoops with two bikes. On the roof BOWLING in huge cherry neon letters on a teal lattice frame (a soft glow behind them at night, steady, no flicker), the giant pin (an 8 m lathe, two red stripes at its neck) and its ball (violet swirl, three holes, rocking a little), floodlit at night. The wall facing the office carries a big mural: STRIKE! in a sunburst, a ball rolling into the pins, boomerangs. Built into the street's outlook (`bowlingOut` in `world/bowling/index.ts`), so it's there from every floor's street, the windows and the roof; one coarse collider for the house. Nothing of the city stands on its block or against its walls: the street furniture keeps 1.4 m off it (`built` in `shared/streetside.ts`), the golf balls and the scenic loop's trees keep off it (`neighbourBoxes`).

**The place.** **E** at the doors goes in (`floor.go` to `BOWLING`); **E** at the doors inside comes back out onto the street of the floor you came from (`sessionStorage`), in front of the doors; a reload brings you back in (`backInPlace`); taking the last floor off the building sends people in there to the lobby (`office/floors.ts`). People from every floor meet in there; whereabouts say where by zone (`bowlingWhereabouts`: at the counter, in the lounge, on the lanes, in the karaoke bar, at the mini golf), the people list says 🎳 Bowling, the minimap shows them at the house. Guests and party guests may do all of it.

**Inside** (`client/bowling/place.ts`, `BowlingPlace`; the room in `world/bowling/`, built the first time anyone goes in): a 90s alley carpet over the whole floor (`carpet.ts`: neon squiggles, triangles, rings, zigzags and confetti on deep navy, drawn once into two canvases, its colours and the shapes alone, which glow under UV), walls with a teal wainscot under a chrome rail, cream above, a band of retro stripes that glows under UV (plain dark inside the mini golf room, whose walls and look are its half's), a dark ceiling with Warren trusses across the room every 5 m, house light panels under them and violet UV tubes beside those and along the walls. The glass doors back out with AUSGANG over them and the house's name in pink neon above. Parts are built into the room with it (`bowlingParts(onJoin)`; a part joining later is built at once), get every message, every frame inside, `place(inside)` and `lights(...)` (once on coming in, and on every switch); E and the hint go to them for anything that isn't the house's. A part may use its own kind (defined in its own file) or the house's generic `bowlingpart`, whose E and hint go to the parts' `use`/`hint`. The room's colliders, interactables and the crosshair's pickables (the whole room group) work as the padel hall's.

**The counter** (`ZONES.counter`, `world/bowling/counter.ts`, laid out in `shared/bowling-house.ts`): a cherry-red counter with a chrome band, ribbed chrome, starbursts and a boomerang formica top facing the lanes, an underglow that goes aqua in cosmic bowling, three beer taps, a cream till, ketchup and mayo; the back bar with the fryer (its oil shimmers), the chip warmer under its lamp and the glasses; red pendant lamps; THEKE on a menu board hanging over it; three bar stools at its west end; Toni behind it (a `Person`, looking up at you when you come close). **E** at the counter orders (`ui.ts`, a window with ✕): Bier vom Fass (the Zwickl), Radler, Spezi, Cola, Pommes rot-weiß, Currywurst, Nachos, the shops', the fridge's and the kiosk's things, so held anywhere and along to every floor; the tap, the fryer and the till play, Toni says something, and it's in your hand like the cinema's snacks (`served`). Had enough beer and the beers are off the menu for now. **The shoe rental**: the shelf of pairs by size (36–47) against the wall behind the counter, SCHUHVERLEIH in pink neon over it; **E** at the end of the counter picks your size (a small window: the sizes, giving them back). The office keeps who wears which size (`bowling.shoes`, `BowlingHouse`); everyone in the centre sees two-tone bowling shoes on your feet (`client/bowling/shoes.ts` puts a pair on each leg, `world/bowling/shoes.ts` the model, its tones by size). Leaving the centre (or the office) gives them back (`bowlingHooks.leaving`, `closed`).

**The lounge** (`ZONES.lounge`, `world/bowling/lounge.ts`): a booth of teal vinyl sofas round a kidney table (popcorn, two beers), another sofa east of the way to the mini golf room's door (`GOLF_WAY` stays clear), two arcade cabinets playing their attract mode (STAR BLASTER, PIN PANIC, redrawn on a canvas) and a jukebox-style arch light whose bubble tubes run through the colours against the mini golf room's wall, an atomic floor lamp, plants. **E** on a sofa sits you down (walk off to get up): like the cinema's seats nobody tells the office, every page sits down whoever stands still on a sofa's seat. **The cosmic bowling switch**: a big lever on a hazard-striped pedestal under a COSMIC BOWLING sign. **E** flips it for the whole centre (`bowling.lights`, at most one flip in 1.5 s); everyone inside gets it (and the state on coming in, `bowling.house`): the house lights drop, then the UV swells in (`world/bowling/lighting.ts`, `HouseLighting`: tints and levels between the two, the house first, the UV after), the carpet, the stripes, the starbursts and the pedestal fluoresce, the scene's light goes deep violet (`mood`), a mirror ball over the lounge throws 90 coloured spots that sweep over the floor, walls and ceiling (one instanced mesh, each spot where its ray meets the room) and laser beams fan out under it (`world/bowling/cosmic.ts`; none of it goes into the mini golf room), and the lever goes down. Every part's `lights(...)` is called.

**Sound** (`client/bowling/sound.ts`, `bowling(kind)` and `setBowling(level, cosmic)` in `sound/index.ts`): the doors (a whoosh and two notes), the lever's clunk and a bass drop with a shimmer (cosmic), or the clunk and the tubes ticking on with a hum (lights back), the tap, the fryer's sizzle, the till's ka-ching, shoes on the counter; inside, the air: a murmur, balls rolling down a lane far off and the pins going now and then, soft muzak of slow chords, and in cosmic bowling a low pulse. It fades away by itself outside.
